+fn embed_mesh_launch(here: &Path) {
+    let source = here.join("src/launch.hlsl");
+    let out = PathBuf::from(std::env::var_os("OUT_DIR").expect("Cargo output directory"))
+        .join("launch.dxil");
+    println!("cargo:rerun-if-changed={}", source.display());
+    println!("cargo:rerun-if-env-changed=VICTORY_MESH_LAUNCH_DXC");
+    if let Some(dxc) = std::env::var_os("VICTORY_MESH_LAUNCH_DXC") {
+        if Path::new(&dxc).is_file() {
+            println!("cargo:rerun-if-changed={}", Path::new(&dxc).display());
+        }
+        let compiled = std::process::Command::new(dxc)
+            .args(["-nologo", "-T", "as_6_6", "-E", "main", "-Fo"])
+            .arg(&out)
+            .arg(&source)
+            .status()
+            .expect("run VICTORY_MESH_LAUNCH_DXC");
+        assert!(compiled.success(), "mesh launch HLSL compilation failed");
+    } else {
+        // An ordinary cross-build can compile the same source at runtime.
+        std::fs::write(out, []).expect("initialize empty mesh launch embedding");
+    }
+}

+/// The unsigned integer format with the layout of a 16-bit float or
+/// signed integer format (one, two or four channels), if `format` is one.
+pub(crate) fn half_format(format: u32) -> Option<u32> {
+    match format {
+        // R16G16B16A16_FLOAT and _SINT: R16G16B16A16_UINT.
+        10 | 14 => Some(12),
+        // R16G16_FLOAT and _SINT: R16G16_UINT.
+        34 | 38 => Some(36),
+        // R16_FLOAT and _SINT: R16_UINT.
+        54 | 59 => Some(57),
+        _ => None,
+    }
+}
+
+/// `view` as a shader is shown it where it moves a buffer's 16-bit
+/// channels as unsigned integers
+/// (`victory_shader::emit::HostShader::half_views`): a buffer view in a
+/// 16-bit float or signed format, in the unsigned format of the same
+/// layout, through which every 16-bit pattern is read and stored as it
+/// is. `None` for every other view: the title's own serves (an unsigned
+/// one already carries the bits; a texture can only be viewed in the
+/// formats it was made for).
+pub(crate) fn half_view(view: &View) -> Option<View> {
+    // Buffer views: dimension 1 in the title's description of either kind.
+    const BUFFER: u32 = 1;
+    match view {
+        View::Shader(resource, Some(desc)) if desc[1] == BUFFER => {
+            let mut desc = *desc;
+            desc[0] = half_format(desc[0])?;
+            Some(View::Shader(*resource, Some(desc)))
+        }
+        View::Unordered(resource, counter, Some(desc)) if desc[1] == BUFFER => {
+            let mut desc = *desc;
+            desc[0] = half_format(desc[0])?;
+            Some(View::Unordered(*resource, *counter, Some(desc)))
+        }
+        View::Placed(unordered, resource, desc) if resource[0] == BUFFER && desc[1] == BUFFER => {
+            let mut desc = *desc;
+            desc[0] = half_format(desc[0])?;
+            Some(View::Placed(*unordered, *resource, desc))
+        }
+        _ => None,
+    }
+}
+

+//! Compiling our own HLSL at run time.
+//!
+//! A few of the host's passes are written in HLSL and compiled when the host
+//! starts: the indirect-argument clamp ([`crate::indirect`]), the compare of
+//! copies in the card's memory and the depth-plane twins ([`crate::host`]),
+//! and the in-place probe ([`crate::inplace`]). The system's compiler
+//! (`D3DCompile`, `d3dcompiler_47`) is tried first, as it always was. Under
+//! Wine and Proton that is vkd3d-shader, which refuses some of this source
+//! outright (`E5005: Method 'Load' is not defined on type
+//! 'RWByteAddressBuffer'`), and every caller then gives up: no argument
+//! clamp, so indirect executions are dropped; no card compare, so card
+//! buffers silently switch off; no twins. It also compiles the rest of the
+//! source but drops the root signature the shader asks for, so
+//! `CreateRootSignature` over its bytes fails and the twins still have no
+//! pipeline. So the same source is compiled with DXC (`dxcompiler.dll`) as
+//! shader model 6.0 when the system compiler refuses it, or leaves the root
+//! signature out of a shader that asked for one. The root signature stays
+//! inside the shader, so the callers see only bytes, and on Windows, where
+//! `D3DCompile` succeeds and keeps the root signature, nothing changes.
+//!
+//! DXC is looked for in `VICTORY_DXC` (the DLL or its folder), beside the
+//! `dxil.dll` that shader validation uses (the Windows SDK ships the two
+//! together), then the executable's folder and System32. A container DXC
+//! compiled without finding `dxil.dll` carries a zero digest, which D3D12
+//! refuses: such containers are signed here (`sign`), `VICTORY_DXIL` naming
+//! the validator if it is somewhere else.
+
+use std::path::PathBuf;
+
+use windows::Win32::Foundation::HMODULE;
+use windows::Win32::Graphics::Direct3D::Dxc::{
+    CLSID_DxcCompiler, DXC_CP_UTF8, DxcBuffer, IDxcCompiler3, IDxcIncludeHandler, IDxcResult,
+};
+use windows::Win32::System::LibraryLoader::{
+    GetModuleFileNameW, GetProcAddress, LOAD_LIBRARY_SEARCH_APPLICATION_DIR,
+    LOAD_LIBRARY_SEARCH_DEFAULT_DIRS, LOAD_LIBRARY_SEARCH_DLL_LOAD_DIR,
+    LOAD_LIBRARY_SEARCH_SYSTEM32, LoadLibraryExW,
+};
+use windows::core::{GUID, HSTRING, Interface, PCWSTR, s, w};
+
+/// Compiles function `entry` of `source` for `target` (e.g. `cs_5_1`), with
+/// the system's compiler or, if it refuses or leaves the root signature out,
+/// with DXC (see the module docs).
+pub(crate) fn compile(source: &str, entry: &str, target: &str) -> windows::core::Result<Vec<u8>> {
+    let bytes = match system(source, entry, target) {
+        Ok(bytes) => bytes,
+        Err(e) => {
+            tracing::info!(
+                error = %e,
+                entry,
+                target,
+                "the system compiler refused one of our shaders: trying DXC"
+            );
+            return dxc(source, entry, target)
+                .inspect_err(|e| tracing::warn!(error = %e, entry, target, "DXC failed too"));
+        }
+    };
+    // Wine's compiler takes the source but drops the root signature it asks
+    // for, and D3D12 refuses those bytes. A shader with no root signature of
+    // its own (a vertex shader whose pixel shader carries it) keeps the
+    // system's bytes: neither compiler has one to give.
+    if !asks_for_root_signature(source, entry) || carries_root_signature(&bytes) {
+        return Ok(bytes);
+    }
+    tracing::info!(
+        entry,
+        target,
+        "the system compiler left the root signature out of one of our shaders: trying DXC"
+    );
+    match dxc(source, entry, target) {
+        Ok(with_signature) if carries_root_signature(&with_signature) => Ok(with_signature),
+        Ok(_) => Ok(bytes),
+        Err(e) => {
+            tracing::warn!(error = %e, entry, target, "DXC failed too");
+            Ok(bytes)
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+/// Whether the container holds the root signature the shader asked for: both
+/// compilers store it as the chunk `RTS0`.
+fn carries_root_signature(bytes: &[u8]) -> bool {
+    bytes.windows(4).any(|chunk| chunk == b"RTS0")
+}
+
+/// Whether the declaration of `entry` asks for a root signature. A source can
+/// hold several entries (a test compiles `corner`, which carries the
+/// signature, and `lit`, which does not), and only the entry's own attribute
+/// should send it to DXC. An attribute this cannot tie to a declaration counts
+/// as the entry's: the answer only decides whether a second compile is
+/// attempted, so it errs towards compiling again.
+fn asks_for_root_signature(source: &str, entry: &str) -> bool {
+    if !source.contains("RootSignature") {
+        return false;
+    }
+    let mut from = 0;
+    let mut found = false;
+    while let Some(at) = source[from..].find("[RootSignature") {
+        let at = from + at;
+        from = at + 1;
+        found = true;
+        // Skip this attribute and any others between it and the declaration.
+        let mut decl = &source[at..];
+        while let Some(attribute) = decl.trim_start().strip_prefix('[') {
+            let Some(close) = attribute.find(']') else {
+                return true;
+            };
+            decl = &attribute[close + 1..];
+        }
+        // The declared function's name is the word before its `(`.
+        let name = decl
+            .trim_start()
+            .split_once('(')
+            .and_then(|(head, _)| head.split_whitespace().last());
+        match name {
+            Some(name) if name == entry => return true,
+            Some(_) => {}
+            None => return true,
+        }
+    }
+    // A mention outside an attribute (`#define`) keeps the conservative
+    // answer.
+    !found
+}
+
+/// `D3DCompile`: the system's compiler, which on Windows compiles all of
+/// this source.
+fn system(source: &str, entry: &str, target: &str) -> windows::core::Result<Vec<u8>> {
+    use windows::Win32::Graphics::Direct3D::Fxc::D3DCompile;
+    use windows::Win32::Graphics::Direct3D::ID3DBlob;
+    use windows::core::PCSTR;
+    // A `PCSTR` ends at its first zero: an interior zero truncates the name,
+    // which is what the compiler would read anyway.
+    let entry = format!("{entry}\0");
+    let target = format!("{target}\0");
+    let mut code: Option<ID3DBlob> = None;
+    let mut errors: Option<ID3DBlob> = None;
+    // SAFETY: the source and names live through the call; out pointers ours.
+    let compiled = unsafe {
+        D3DCompile(
+            source.as_ptr().cast(),
+            source.len(),
+            s!("victory"),
+            None,
+            None,
+            PCSTR(entry.as_ptr()),
+            PCSTR(target.as_ptr()),
+            0,
+            0,
+            &mut code,
+            Some(&mut errors),
+        )
+    };
+    if let Err(e) = compiled {
+        if let Some(errors) = errors {
+            // SAFETY: the blob's own bytes.
+            let text = unsafe {
+                std::slice::from_raw_parts(
+                    errors.GetBufferPointer().cast::<u8>(),
+                    errors.GetBufferSize(),
+                )
+            };
+            tracing::info!(
+                "one of our shaders: {}",
+                String::from_utf8_lossy(text).trim()
+            );
+        }
+        return Err(e);
+    }
+    let code = code.ok_or_else(windows::core::Error::empty)?;
+    // SAFETY: the blob's own bytes.
+    let bytes = unsafe {
+        std::slice::from_raw_parts(code.GetBufferPointer().cast::<u8>(), code.GetBufferSize())
+    };
+    Ok(bytes.to_vec())
+}
+
+/// Compiles with DXC: the same source as shader model 6.0 (5.1 is the
+/// oldest profile DXC knows, and 6.0 takes everything 5.1 does), root
+/// signature inside.
+fn dxc(source: &str, entry: &str, target: &str) -> windows::core::Result<Vec<u8>> {
+    type Create = unsafe extern "system" fn(
+        *const GUID,
+        *const GUID,
+        *mut *mut std::ffi::c_void,
+    ) -> windows::core::HRESULT;
+    let library = library()?;
+    let profile = match target.strip_suffix("_5_1") {
+        Some(kind) => format!("{kind}_6_0"),
+        None => target.to_string(),
+    };
+    let (target_flag, profile, entry) = (wide("-T"), wide(&profile), wide(entry));
+    let arguments = [
+        PCWSTR(target_flag.as_ptr()),
+        PCWSTR(profile.as_ptr()),
+        w!("-E"),
+        PCWSTR(entry.as_ptr()),
+        w!("-O3"),
+    ];
+    // SAFETY: loading the compiler and calling its documented entry point
+    // with out pointers of ours; the source, the arguments and the strings
+    // they point at outlive the calls.
+    unsafe {
+        let create: Create = std::mem::transmute(
+            GetProcAddress(library, s!("DxcCreateInstance"))
+                .ok_or_else(windows::core::Error::from_thread)?,
+        );
+        let mut compiler = std::ptr::null_mut();
+        create(&CLSID_DxcCompiler, &IDxcCompiler3::IID, &mut compiler).ok()?;
+        let compiler = IDxcCompiler3::from_raw(compiler);
+        let buffer = DxcBuffer {
+            Ptr: source.as_ptr().cast(),
+            Size: source.len(),
+            Encoding: DXC_CP_UTF8.0,
+        };
+        let result: IDxcResult =
+            compiler.Compile(&buffer, Some(&arguments), None::<&IDxcIncludeHandler>)?;
+        if let Err(e) = result.GetStatus()?.ok() {
+            if let Ok(errors) = result.GetErrorBuffer() {
+                // SAFETY: the blob's own bytes.
+                let text = std::slice::from_raw_parts(
+                    errors.GetBufferPointer().cast::<u8>(),
+                    errors.GetBufferSize(),
+                );
+                tracing::warn!(
+                    "one of our shaders: DXC: {}",
+                    String::from_utf8_lossy(text).trim()
+                );
+            }
+            return Err(e);
+        }
+        let blob = result.GetResult()?;
+        // SAFETY: the blob's own bytes.
+        let mut bytes =
+            std::slice::from_raw_parts(blob.GetBufferPointer().cast::<u8>(), blob.GetBufferSize())
+                .to_vec();
+        sign(library, &mut bytes)?;
+        Ok(bytes)
+    }
+}
+
+/// Where `dxcompiler.dll` is looked for: `VICTORY_DXC` (the DLL or its
+/// folder), beside the `dxil.dll` shader validation uses (the SDK ships
+/// them together), then the executable's folder and System32.
+fn library() -> windows::core::Result<HMODULE> {
+    let mut paths: Vec<PathBuf> = Vec::new();
+    if let Some(p) = std::env::var_os("VICTORY_DXC").map(PathBuf::from) {
+        paths.push(if p.is_dir() {
+            p.join("dxcompiler.dll")
+        } else {
+            p
+        });
+    }
+    if let Some(dir) = victory_shader::emit::validator::Validator::default_path()
+        .and_then(|p| p.parent().map(|d| d.to_path_buf()))
+    {
+        paths.push(dir.join("dxcompiler.dll"));
+    }
+    for path in paths.iter().filter(|p| p.is_file()) {
+        // SAFETY: a NUL-terminated path; its folder (`dxil.dll`) is searched
+        // first.
+        if let Ok(module) = unsafe {
+            LoadLibraryExW(
+                &HSTRING::from(path.as_path()),
+                None,
+                LOAD_LIBRARY_SEARCH_DLL_LOAD_DIR | LOAD_LIBRARY_SEARCH_DEFAULT_DIRS,
+            )
+        } {
+            return Ok(module);
+        }
+    }
+    // SAFETY: a bare name, searched only in the executable's folder and
+    // System32.
+    unsafe {
+        LoadLibraryExW(
+            w!("dxcompiler.dll"),
+            None,
+            LOAD_LIBRARY_SEARCH_APPLICATION_DIR | LOAD_LIBRARY_SEARCH_SYSTEM32,
+        )
+    }
+}
+
+/// Older DXC DLLs compile without finding `dxil.dll`, and the container then
+/// carries a zero digest, which D3D12 refuses: such a container is validated
+/// (and signed) here.
+fn sign(compiler: HMODULE, bytes: &mut [u8]) -> windows::core::Result<()> {
+    use std::os::windows::ffi::OsStringExt;
+
+    use victory_shader::emit::validator::Validator;
+    use windows::Win32::Foundation::E_FAIL;
+
+    if bytes
+        .get(4..20)
+        .is_some_and(|digest| digest.iter().any(|&b| b != 0))
+    {
+        return Ok(());
+    }
+    let beside_compiler = || {
+        let mut path = vec![0; 32_768];
+        // SAFETY: the compiler's module is loaded and the buffer is ours.
+        let len = unsafe { GetModuleFileNameW(Some(compiler), &mut path) } as usize;
+        if len == 0 || len >= path.len() {
+            return None;
+        }
+        let compiler = PathBuf::from(std::ffi::OsString::from_wide(&path[..len]));
+        let validator = compiler.with_file_name("dxil.dll");
+        validator.is_file().then_some(validator)
+    };
+    let explicit = std::env::var_os("VICTORY_DXIL").map(PathBuf::from);
+    let path = explicit
+        .or_else(beside_compiler)
+        .or_else(Validator::default_path);
+    Validator::load(path.as_deref())
+        .and_then(|mut validator| validator.validate(bytes))
+        .map_err(|error| {
+            windows::core::Error::new(E_FAIL, format!("cannot sign a DXC shader: {error}"))
+        })
+}
+
+/// A NUL-terminated UTF-16 copy of `text`, for DXC's arguments.
+fn wide(text: &str) -> Vec<u16> {
+    text.encode_utf16().chain([0]).collect()
+}

+#[cfg(test)]

+    //! The fallback, and the bytes it makes: under Wine the system compiler
+    //! refuses this source and DXC compiles it; on Windows `D3DCompile`
+    //! compiles it, and either way D3D12 must take what comes out.
+

+        D3D12_COMPUTE_PIPELINE_STATE_DESC, D3D12_SHADER_BYTECODE, D3D12CreateDevice, ID3D12Device,

+    /// A shader that loads through an `RWByteAddressBuffer` (vkd3d-shader
+    /// refuses the `Load`) and asks for a root signature (which vkd3d-shader
+    /// would drop): the shape the in-place probe has.
+    const SOURCE: &str = r#"
+#define RS "UAV(u0)"
+RWByteAddressBuffer b : register(u0);
+[RootSignature(RS)]
+[numthreads(1, 1, 1)]
+void main() { b.Store(8, b.Load(0) + b.Load(4)); }
+"#;
+
+    #[test]
+    fn a_shader_the_system_compiler_refuses_is_compiled_by_dxc() {
+        match system(SOURCE, "main", "cs_5_1") {
+            Ok(_) => println!("the system compiler compiled it: DXC is not exercised here"),
+            Err(e) => println!("the system compiler refused it: {e}"),
+        }
+        let bytes = compile(SOURCE, "main", "cs_5_1").expect("a compiler must take the shader");
+        let magic = std::str::from_utf8(&bytes[..4]).unwrap_or("?");
+        println!("container {magic}, {} bytes", bytes.len());
+        assert!(
+            matches!(magic, "DXIL" | "DXBC"),
+            "not a shader container: {magic}"
+        );
+        assert!(
+            bytes[4..20].iter().any(|&b| b != 0),
+            "the container carries no digest: D3D12 would refuse it"
+        );
+        assert!(
+            carries_root_signature(&bytes),
+            "the container carries no root signature, which the shader asks for"
+        );
+        let Some(device) = device() else {
+            println!("no D3D12 device here: the root signature and pipeline are not checked");
+            return;
+        };
+        // SAFETY: the root signature is inside the shader; the description
+        // points at live bytes and never releases its pointer.
+        unsafe {
+            let root: ID3D12RootSignature = device
+                .CreateRootSignature(0, &bytes)
+                .expect("D3D12 must take the root signature inside the shader");
+            device
+                .CreateComputePipelineState::<ID3D12PipelineState>(
+                    &D3D12_COMPUTE_PIPELINE_STATE_DESC {
+                        pRootSignature: std::mem::transmute_copy(&root),
+                        CS: D3D12_SHADER_BYTECODE {
+                            pShaderBytecode: bytes.as_ptr().cast(),
+                            BytecodeLength: bytes.len(),
+                        },
+                        ..Default::default()
+                    },
+                )
+                .expect("the shader must make a compute pipeline");
+        }
+        println!("D3D12 took the root signature and made the pipeline");
+    }
+
+    #[test]
+    fn card_compare_shader_keeps_its_root_signature_under_wine() {
+        let bytes = compile(crate::host::CARD_COMPARE, "main", "cs_5_1")
+            .expect("the card compare shader must compile");
+        assert!(carries_root_signature(&bytes));
+        let device = device().expect("a D3D12 device for the compare pipeline regression");
+        // SAFETY: the compiled shader owns its root signature; bytes stay live.
+        unsafe {
+            let root: ID3D12RootSignature = device.CreateRootSignature(0, &bytes).unwrap();
+            device.CreateComputePipelineState::<ID3D12PipelineState>(
+                &D3D12_COMPUTE_PIPELINE_STATE_DESC {
+                    pRootSignature: std::mem::transmute_copy(&root),
+                    CS: D3D12_SHADER_BYTECODE {
+                        pShaderBytecode: bytes.as_ptr().cast(),
+                        BytecodeLength: bytes.len(),
+                    },
+                    ..Default::default()
+                },
+            ).expect("the compare shader must create a pipeline");
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// A source can hold several entries: only the entry carrying the
+    /// attribute asks for a root signature, so only it is retried with DXC.
+    #[test]
+    fn a_root_signature_belongs_to_the_entry_it_sits_on() {
+        const TWO: &str = r#"
+#define RS "UAV(u0)"
+[RootSignature(RS)]
+[numthreads(1, 1, 1)]

+        assert!(!asks_for_root_signature(
+            "float4 lit() : SV_Target { return 1.0; }",
+            "lit"
+        ));
+    }

+        assert!(!carries_root_signature(&bytes));
+    }

+// The request is announced only after MakeResident succeeds. A later
+// arrival must wait for a new fence even if it arrives during this wait.
+fn cover_mappings(
+    mapped_through: &mut u64,
+    request: Option<u64>,
+    requested: &AtomicU64,
+    wait: impl FnOnce() -> windows::core::Result<()>,
+) -> windows::core::Result<bool> {
+    if request.is_some_and(|id| id <= *mapped_through) {
+        return Ok(false);
+    }
+    let cutoff = request.map(|_| requested.load(Ordering::Acquire));
+    wait()?;
+    if let Some(cutoff) = cutoff {
+        *mapped_through = cutoff;
+    }
+    Ok(true)
+}
+
+#[test]
+fn a_mapping_fence_only_covers_requests_announced_before_it() {
+    use windows::Win32::Foundation::E_FAIL;
+
+    let mut mapped_through = 0;
+    let announced = AtomicU64::new(2);
+    let waiting = cover_mappings(&mut mapped_through, Some(1), &announced, || {
+        announced.fetch_add(1, Ordering::AcqRel);
+        Ok(())
+    });
+    assert!(waiting.unwrap());
+    assert_eq!(mapped_through, 2);
+    assert!(!cover_mappings(&mut mapped_through, Some(2), &announced, || panic!("already covered")).unwrap());
+    assert!(cover_mappings(&mut mapped_through, Some(3), &announced, || Err(E_FAIL.into())).is_err());
+    assert_eq!(mapped_through, 2);
+    assert!(cover_mappings(&mut mapped_through, Some(3), &announced, || Ok(())).unwrap());
+    assert_eq!(mapped_through, 3);
+    assert!(cover_mappings(&mut mapped_through, None, &announced, || Ok(())).unwrap());
+}

+/// use the same result slots while their original byte bounds stay intact.
+fn reserve_compare(
+    last: &mut Option<CompareSlots>, next: &mut u32, capacity: u32, first: u32, pieces: u32, join: bool,
+) -> Option<u32> {
+    let end = first.checked_add(pieces)?;
+    let run = (*last).filter(|run| join && first >= run.first && first <= run.end);
+    let added = run.map_or(pieces, |run| end.saturating_sub(run.end));
+    let after = next.checked_add(added).filter(|&after| after <= capacity)?;
+    let run = run.map_or(CompareSlots { first, end, at: *next }, |run| CompareSlots { end: end.max(run.end), ..run });
+    *next = after;
+    *last = Some(run);
+    Some(run.at + (first - run.first))
+}
+
+fn extend_compare(span: &mut crate::shadow::CardSpan, at: u32, next: crate::shadow::CardSpan, next_at: u32) -> bool {
+    if next.first < span.first || next.first > span.first + span.pieces || next_at != at + (next.first - span.first) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    span.pieces = span.pieces.max(next.first - span.first + next.pieces);
+    true
+}
+
+#[test]
+fn shared_card_results_cover_exactly_the_pieces_reserved() {
+    use crate::shadow::CardSpan;
+    let mut last = None;
+    let mut taken = 7;
+    assert_eq!(reserve_compare(&mut last, &mut taken, 11, 40, 3, true), Some(7));
+    assert_eq!(reserve_compare(&mut last, &mut taken, 11, 41, 4, true), None);
+    assert_eq!(taken, 10);
+    assert_eq!(reserve_compare(&mut last, &mut taken, 11, 42, 2, true), Some(9));
+    assert_eq!(reserve_compare(&mut last, &mut taken, 11, 44, 1, true), None);
+    assert_eq!(reserve_compare(&mut last, &mut taken, 11, 43, 1, false), None);
+
+    let mut seed = 7u32;
+    let mut random = || {
+        seed = seed.wrapping_mul(1664525).wrapping_add(1013904223);
+        seed >> 18
+    };
+    for _ in 0..4000 {
+        let (mut previous, mut next) = (None, 5u32);
+        let capacity = next + random() % 120;
+        let mut first = random() % 200;
+        let mut expected = std::collections::BTreeMap::new();
+        let mut dispatches: Vec<(CardSpan, u32)> = Vec::new();
+        for _ in 0..32 {
+            first += random() % 7;
+            let pieces = random() % 8 + 1;
+            if let Some(at) = reserve_compare(&mut previous, &mut next, capacity, first, pieces, true) {
+                let span = CardSpan { first, pieces, from: 0, to: 0, skew: 16 };
+                for i in 0..pieces {
+                    assert_eq!(*expected.entry(first + i).or_insert(at + i), at + i);
+                }
+                if !dispatches.last_mut().is_some_and(|(previous, address)| extend_compare(previous, *address, span, at)) {
+                    dispatches.push((span, at));
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        let mut actual = std::collections::BTreeMap::new();
+        let mut results = std::collections::BTreeSet::new();
+        for (span, at) in dispatches {
+            for i in 0..span.pieces {
+                assert!(at + i < capacity && results.insert(at + i));
+                assert!(actual.insert(span.first + i, at + i).is_none());
+            }
+        }
+        assert_eq!(actual, expected);
+        assert_eq!(results.len() as u32, next-5);
+    }
+}

+#[test]
+fn ranges_sharing_a_gpu_compare_keep_the_same_changed_pieces() {
+    use crate::shadow::CardSpan;
+    const SIZE: usize = 3 * 65_536;
+    let host = host().expect("D3D12 host");
+    let work = host.make_card_work().expect("compare pipeline");
+    let (source, data) = host.shared_buffer(SIZE as u64).expect("mapped source");
+    let hashes = host.uav_buffer(3 * 16).expect("hashes UAV");
+    let span = |first, pieces| CardSpan { from: 0, to: SIZE as u32, first, pieces, skew: 0 };
+    let run = |span: CardSpan, at, mode| {
+        let list = host.new_list().expect("compare list");
+        // SAFETY: buffers and their spans live through this fenced list.
+        unsafe {
+            list.list.SetComputeRootSignature(&work.root);
+            list.list.SetPipelineState(&work.pso);
+            list.list.SetComputeRootShaderResourceView(1, source.GetGPUVirtualAddress());
+            list.list.SetComputeRootUnorderedAccessView(2, hashes.GetGPUVirtualAddress());
+            list.list.SetComputeRootUnorderedAccessView(3, work.differs.0.GetGPUVirtualAddress());
+            card_pass(&list.list, &span, SIZE as u32, at, mode);
+        }
+        list.close();
+        host.execute(&[&list]);
+    };
+    // SAFETY: the mapping has SIZE bytes and we wait for all GPU work.
+    let data = unsafe { std::slice::from_raw_parts_mut(data, SIZE) };
+    let flags = || {
+        // SAFETY: the queries wrote the result dwords before the fence.
+        unsafe { std::slice::from_raw_parts(work.differs.1 as *const u32, 4) }
+    };
+    let original = [span(0, 2), span(1, 2)];
+    data.fill(7);
+    run(span(0, 3), 0, 0);
+    data[13] = 19;
+    data[65_536 + 11] = 81;
+    run(original[0], 0, 2);
+    run(original[1], 2, 2);
+    let baseline = flags().to_vec();
+    assert_eq!(baseline.iter().copied().map(torn::flagged).collect::<Vec<_>>(), [true, true, false, false]);
+
+    data.fill(7);
+    run(span(0, 3), 0, 0);
+    data[13] = 19;
+    data[65_536 + 11] = 81;
+    let (mut previous, mut next) = (None, 0);
+    let slots = original.map(|range| reserve_compare(&mut previous, &mut next, 4, range.first, range.pieces, true).unwrap());
+    assert_eq!((slots, next), ([0, 1], 3));
+    let mut joined = original[0];
+    assert!(extend_compare(&mut joined, 0, original[1], 1));
+    run(joined, 0, 2);
+    assert_eq!(flags()[..3], [baseline[0], baseline[1], baseline[3]]);
+    run(joined, 0, 1);
+    assert_eq!(flags()[..3].iter().copied().map(torn::flagged).collect::<Vec<_>>(), [false; 3]);
+    eprintln!("shared compare reproduced flags and stored hashes across both ranges");
+}

+/// Join only adjacent bytes of both buffers, in their original order.
+fn readback_runs<T: Copy>(
+    ranges: impl IntoIterator<Item = (T, usize, usize, u64)>,
+    join: bool,
+    same: impl Fn(T, T) -> bool,
+    mut copy: impl FnMut(T, usize, usize, u64),
+) -> usize {
+    let mut ranges = ranges.into_iter().peekable();
+    let mut commands = 0;
+    while let Some((source, start, mut end, target)) = ranges.next() {
+        if join {
+            while let Some(&(next, lo, hi, at)) = ranges.peek() {
+                if lo != end || target.checked_add((end - start) as u64) != Some(at) || !same(source, next) {
+                    break;
+                }
+                end = hi;
+                ranges.next();
+            }
+        }
+        copy(source, start, end, target);
+        commands += 1;
+    }
+    commands
+}
+
+fn join_readbacks() -> bool {
+    static ON: OnceLock<bool> = OnceLock::new();
+    *ON.get_or_init(|| crate::switch::on("VICTORY_FETCH_JOIN"))
+}
+
+#[cfg(feature = "diagnostics")]
+static READBACK_RUNS: [AtomicU64; 2] = [const { AtomicU64::new(0) }; 2];
+
+#[cfg(feature = "diagnostics")]
+fn note_readback_runs(pieces: usize, commands: usize) {
+    READBACK_RUNS[0].fetch_add(pieces as u64, Ordering::Relaxed);
+    READBACK_RUNS[1].fetch_add(commands as u64, Ordering::Relaxed);
+}
+
+#[cfg(feature = "diagnostics")]
+fn readback_runs_line(frames: f64) {
+    let [pieces, commands] = READBACK_RUNS.each_ref().map(|n| n.swap(0, Ordering::Relaxed));
+    if pieces != 0 {
+        tracing::info!(
+            "card readback per frame (VICTORY_FETCH_JOIN={}): {:.1} pieces in {:.1} copy commands",
+            u8::from(join_readbacks()),
+            pieces as f64 / frames,
+            commands as f64 / frames,
+        );
+    }
+}
+

+        let first: Vec<u8> = (0..SIZE).map(|i| (i.wrapping_mul(13) ^ (i >> 9)) as u8).collect();
+        let second: Vec<u8> = (0..SIZE).map(|i| (i.wrapping_mul(31) ^ (i >> 7) ^ 211) as u8).collect();
+        // SAFETY: CPU writes to our live mappings before they are submitted.

+            expected[at as usize..at as usize + hi - lo].copy_from_slice(&bytes[lo..hi]);
+        }

+            // SAFETY: requests fit inside the given buffers. Wipes happen

+            // has a persistent CPU-readable mapping of SIZE bytes.
+            assert_eq!(unsafe { std::slice::from_raw_parts(view, SIZE) }, expected);
+        }
+        println!("exact adjacent GPU readback parity, six commands reduced to four");
+    }

+//! Verify a bounded producer snapshot with the actual card-compare hash.
+
+use super::*;
+use std::collections::BTreeSet;
+
+#[test]
+fn capture_keeps_compare_results_and_preserves_changed_bytes() {
+    const PIECE: usize = 65_536;
+    const SIZE: usize = 2 * PIECE;
+    const AT: u32 = 3;
+    const COUNT: u32 = 3;
+    const SLOTS: usize = 4;
+    const DWORDS: usize = 4 + COUNT as usize;
+    let host = host().expect("D3D12 host");
+    let ordinary = host.make_card_work().expect("ordinary compare");
+    let (root, pso) = producer_capture::pipeline(host).expect("fused compare shader");
+    let (source, bytes) = host.shared_buffer(SIZE as u64).expect("compared bytes");
+    let hashes = host.uav_buffer(u64::from(COUNT * 16)).expect("hash buffer");
+    let (meta, metadata) = host
+        .shared_buffer((DWORDS * 4) as u64)
+        .expect("slot results");
+    let (snapshot, captured) = host
+        .shared_buffer((SLOTS * PIECE) as u64)
+        .expect("snapshot payload");
+    // SAFETY: private CPU-mapped buffers with every GPU access waited for.
+    let data = unsafe { std::slice::from_raw_parts_mut(bytes, SIZE) };
+    let metas = unsafe { std::slice::from_raw_parts_mut(metadata.cast::<u32>(), DWORDS) };
+    let captures = unsafe { std::slice::from_raw_parts_mut(captured, SLOTS * PIECE) };
+    let run = |skew, mode, capacity: Option<u32>| {
+        let list = host.new_list().expect("open compare list");
+        let capacity_on = capacity.is_some();
+        // SAFETY: root parameters match that pipeline and all buffers live.
+        unsafe {
+            let (root, pso) = if capacity_on {
+                (&root, &pso)
+            } else {
+                (&ordinary.root, &ordinary.pso)
+            };
+            list.list.SetComputeRootSignature(root);
+            list.list.SetPipelineState(pso);
+            list.list
+                .SetComputeRootShaderResourceView(1, source.GetGPUVirtualAddress());
+            list.list
+                .SetComputeRootUnorderedAccessView(2, hashes.GetGPUVirtualAddress());
+            list.list
+                .SetComputeRootUnorderedAccessView(3, ordinary.differs.0.GetGPUVirtualAddress());
+            if let Some(capacity) = capacity {
+                list.list
+                    .SetComputeRootUnorderedAccessView(4, meta.GetGPUVirtualAddress());
+                list.list
+                    .SetComputeRootUnorderedAccessView(5, snapshot.GetGPUVirtualAddress());
+                for (first, pieces) in [(0, 1), (1, COUNT - 1)] {
+                    let values = [
+                        first,
+                        skew,
+                        SIZE as u32,
+                        pieces,
+                        (AT + first) * 4,
+                        mode,
+                        AT,
+                        capacity,
+                    ];
+                    list.list
+                        .SetComputeRoot32BitConstants(0, 8, values.as_ptr().cast(), 0);
+                    list.list.Dispatch(pieces, 1, 1);
+                }
+            } else {
+                let span = crate::shadow::CardSpan {
+                    from: 0,
+                    to: SIZE as u32,
+                    first: 0,
+                    pieces: COUNT,
+                    skew,
+                };
+                card_pass(&list.list, &span, SIZE as u32, AT, mode);
+            }
+        }
+        list.close();
+        host.execute(&[&list]);
+        // SAFETY: waited for the comparison writes if mode is nonzero.
+        unsafe {
+            *((ordinary.differs.1 as *const u32)
+                .add(AT as usize)
+                .cast::<[u32; COUNT as usize]>())
+        }
+    };
+    for skew in [96u32, 97] {
+        let patch = |data: &mut [u8]| {
+            for at in [0, PIECE - skew as usize + 64, SIZE - 1] {
+                data[at] = 59;
+            }
+        };
+        data.fill(7);
+        run(skew, 0, None);
+        patch(data);
+        let expected = data.to_vec();
+        let expected_flags = run(skew, 1, None);
+        assert!(expected_flags.into_iter().all(torn::flagged));
+        for capacity in [0, 1, 3] {
+            data.fill(7);
+            run(skew, 0, None);
+            patch(data);
+            metas.fill(0x75);
+            metas[0] = 0;
+            captures.fill(0xd2);
+            assert_eq!(run(skew, 2, Some(capacity as u32)), expected_flags);
+            data.fill(0x61);
+            let mut occupied = BTreeSet::new();
+            let mut eligible = 0;
+            for (piece, &slot) in metas[4..].iter().enumerate() {
+                let lo = (piece * PIECE).saturating_sub(skew as usize) & !15;
+                let hi = ((piece + 1) * PIECE - skew as usize)
+                    .min(SIZE)
+                    .next_multiple_of(16);
+                if hi - lo > PIECE {
+                    assert_eq!(slot, u32::MAX);
+                    continue;
+                }
+                eligible += 1;
+                if slot == u32::MAX {
+                    assert!(capacity < COUNT as usize);
+                    continue;
+                }
+                let slot = slot as usize;
+                assert!(slot < capacity && occupied.insert(slot));
+                let payload = &captures[slot * PIECE..(slot + 1) * PIECE];
+                assert_eq!(&payload[..hi - lo], &expected[lo..hi]);
+                assert!(payload[hi - lo..].iter().all(|&b| b == 0xd2));
+            }
+            assert_eq!(metas[0] as usize, eligible);
+            assert!(metas[1..4].iter().all(|&word| word == 0x75));
+            assert_eq!(occupied.len(), capacity.min(eligible));
+            assert!(
+                captures[occupied.len() * PIECE..]
+                    .iter()
+                    .all(|&b| b == 0xd2)
+            );
+            data.copy_from_slice(&expected);
+            assert!(run(skew, 1, None).into_iter().all(|d| !torn::flagged(d)));
+        }
+    }
+    eprintln!("producer capture matched compare flags, byte payloads, and bounded fallback");
+}

+//! Snapshot changed card pieces while their producing flight still owns the data.
+
+use super::*;
+
+const PIECE: usize = 65_536;
+const SLOTS: usize = 128;
+const RESULTS: usize = 8192;
+const POOL: usize = 4;
+
+pub(super) fn on() -> bool {
+    static ON: OnceLock<bool> = OnceLock::new();
+    *ON.get_or_init(|| std::env::var_os("VICTORY_PRODUCER_CAPTURE").is_some_and(|v| v == "1"))
+}
+
+pub(super) fn pipeline(
+    host: &Host,
+) -> windows::core::Result<(ID3D12RootSignature, ID3D12PipelineState)> {
+    let source = format!("#define CAPTURE_CARD_RESULT 1\n{CARD_COMPARE}");
+    let code = crate::dxc::compile(&source, "main", "cs_5_1")?;
+    // SAFETY: the shader and its embedded root signature live through creation.
+    unsafe {
+        let root: ID3D12RootSignature = host.device.CreateRootSignature(0, &code)?;
+        let pso = host
+            .device
+            .CreateComputePipelineState(&D3D12_COMPUTE_PIPELINE_STATE_DESC {
+                pRootSignature: std::mem::transmute_copy(&root),
+                CS: D3D12_SHADER_BYTECODE {
+                    pShaderBytecode: code.as_ptr().cast(),
+                    BytecodeLength: code.len(),
+                },
+                ..Default::default()
+            })?;
+        Ok((root, pso))
+    }
+}
+
+pub(super) struct Work {
+    root: ID3D12RootSignature,
+    pso: ID3D12PipelineState,
+    free: Vec<Slot>,
+}
+
+impl Work {
+    pub(super) fn new(host: &Host) -> Option<Self> {
+        if !on()
+            || !relay()
+            || !relay_copy()
+            || torn::on()
+            || crate::land::skip_on()
+            || !crate::land::on()
+        {
+            return None;
+        }
+        let (root, pso) = pipeline(host)
+            .inspect_err(|e| tracing::warn!(error = %e, "producer capture pipeline unavailable"))
+            .ok()?;
+        let free = (0..POOL).map_while(|_| Slot::new(host)).collect::<Vec<_>>();
+        if free.is_empty() {
+            tracing::warn!("no producer capture buffers, card copies use readback");
+            return None;
+        }
+        tracing::info!(
+            slots = free.len(),
+            bytes_per_slot = SLOTS * PIECE,
+            "producer capture enabled"
+        );
+        Some(Self { root, pso, free })
+    }
+
+    pub(super) fn take(&mut self, base: u32, end: u32) -> Option<Capture> {
+        let count = end.checked_sub(base)? as usize;
+        if count == 0 || count > RESULTS {
+            return None;
+        }
+        let slot = self.free.pop()?;
+        // SAFETY: no flight uses a free slot. The GPU visits the first
+        // dword as an atomic counter after this CPU store.
+        unsafe {
+            let words = slot.meta.1 as *mut u32;
+            words.write(0);
+            // If one compare cannot be recorded, a reused result must
+            // still ask for a fetch.
+            words.add(4).write_bytes(0xff, count);
+        }
+        Some(Capture { base, count, slot })
+    }
+
+    pub(super) fn give(&mut self, capture: Capture) {
+        self.free.push(capture.slot);
+    }
+
+    /// # Safety
+    /// The list is open and the slot stays alive until it has finished.
+    pub(super) unsafe fn bind(&self, list: &ID3D12GraphicsCommandList, capture: &Capture) {
+        unsafe {
+            list.SetComputeRootSignature(&self.root);
+            list.SetPipelineState(&self.pso);
+            list.SetComputeRootUnorderedAccessView(4, capture.slot.meta.0.GetGPUVirtualAddress());
+            list.SetComputeRootUnorderedAccessView(
+                5,
+                capture.slot.payload.0.GetGPUVirtualAddress(),
+            );
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+struct Slot {
+    meta: (ID3D12Resource, usize),
+    payload: (ID3D12Resource, usize),
+}
+
+impl Slot {
+    fn new(host: &Host) -> Option<Self> {
+        let (meta, map) = host.shared_buffer((16 + 4 * RESULTS) as u64)?;
+        let (payload, payload_map) = host.shared_buffer((SLOTS * PIECE) as u64)?;
+        Some(Self {
+            meta: (meta, map as usize),
+            payload: (payload, payload_map as usize),
+        })
+    }
+}
+
+pub(super) struct Capture {
+    base: u32,
+    count: usize,
+    slot: Slot,
+}
+
+impl Capture {
+    /// # Safety
+    /// The fused comparison pipeline and its resources are bound. The
+    /// comparison result indexes belong to this flight.
+    pub(super) unsafe fn pass(
+        &self,
+        list: &ID3D12GraphicsCommandList,
+        span: &crate::shadow::CardSpan,
+        size: u32,
+        at: u32,
+        mode: u32,
+    ) {
+        let mut done = 0;
+        while done < span.pieces {
+            let n = (span.pieces - done).min(D3D12_CS_DISPATCH_MAX_THREAD_GROUPS_PER_DIMENSION);
+            let values = [
+                span.first + done,
+                span.skew,
+                size,
+                n,
+                (at + done) * 4,
+                mode,
+                self.base,
+                SLOTS as u32,
+            ];
+            unsafe {
+                list.SetComputeRoot32BitConstants(0, 8, values.as_ptr().cast(), 0);
+                list.Dispatch(n, 1, 1);
+            }
+            done += n;
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// Return one complete batch of captured addresses, or keep the existing
+    /// fetch for the entire batch. No offsets change on failure.
+    ///
+    /// # Safety
+    /// The flight's fence has completed and the capture is retained by it.
+    pub(super) unsafe fn map(
+        &self,
+        ranges: &[CardRange],
+        pieces: &mut [torn::Landed],
+    ) -> Option<usize> {
+        // SAFETY: the slot holds count indexes; they are read after the fence.
+        let slots = unsafe {
+            std::slice::from_raw_parts((self.slot.meta.1 as *const u32).add(4), self.count)
+        };
+        let offsets = pieces
+            .iter()
+            .map(|(shadow, lo, hi, _)| {
+                let piece = shadow.card_piece_of(*lo);
+                let hashed = shadow.card_hashed(piece);
+                // A declared range may only cover a fragment. The capture
+                // includes all the bytes that contributed to that piece's hash.
+                ranges
+                    .iter()
+                    .filter(|r| {
+                        r.compared && !r.picked && std::sync::Arc::ptr_eq(&r.shadow, shadow)
+                    })
+                    .find_map(|r| {
+                        let within = piece
+                            .checked_sub(r.span.first)
+                            .filter(|&i| i < r.span.pieces)?;
+                        let result = (r.at + within).checked_sub(self.base)? as usize;
+                        offset(*slots.get(result)?, hashed, (*lo, *hi))
+                    })
+            })
+            .collect::<Option<Vec<_>>>()?;
+        for (piece, offset) in pieces.iter_mut().zip(offsets) {
+            piece.3 = offset as u64;
+        }
+        Some(self.slot.payload.1)
+    }
+}
+
+fn offset(slot: u32, (from, to): (usize, usize), (lo, hi): (usize, usize)) -> Option<usize> {
+    let slot = slot as usize;
+    (slot < SLOTS && from <= lo && lo < hi && hi <= to && to - from <= PIECE)
+        .then(|| slot * PIECE + lo - from)
+}
+
+#[test]
+fn offsets_reject_uncaptured_misaligned_and_out_of_bounds_pieces() {
+    assert_eq!(
+        offset(3, (32, 32 + PIECE), (112, 496)),
+        Some(3 * PIECE + 80)
+    );
+    assert_eq!(offset(0, (0, 128), (16, 128)), Some(16));
+    assert_eq!(offset(u32::MAX, (32, 224), (32, 80)), None);
+    assert_eq!(offset(SLOTS as u32, (32, 224), (32, 80)), None);
+    assert_eq!(offset(0, (32, 32 + PIECE + 16), (32, 48)), None);
+    assert_eq!(offset(0, (32, 224), (16, 48)), None);
+    assert_eq!(offset(0, (32, 224), (192, 240)), None);
+    assert_eq!(offset(0, (32, 224), (64, 64)), None);
+}
+
+static USED: [AtomicU64; 2] = [const { AtomicU64::new(0) }; 2];
+
+pub(super) fn used(bytes: u64) {
+    USED[0].fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
+    USED[1].fetch_add(bytes, Ordering::Relaxed);
+}
+
+pub(super) fn line(frames: f64) {
+    if on() {
+        let [used, bytes] = USED.each_ref().map(|n| n.swap(0, Ordering::Relaxed));
+        tracing::info!(
+            "producer capture per frame: {:.2} fetches skipped, {:.2} MB landed from the producer",
+            used as f64 / frames,
+            bytes as f64 / frames / 1e6
+        );
+    }
+}
+
+#[test]
+fn slot_results_publish_atomically_and_respect_shared_compare_ranges() {
+    use std::sync::Arc;
+
+    let host = host().expect("D3D12 host");
+    let slot = Slot::new(host).expect("reusable capture slot");
+    let shadow =
+        crate::shadow::Shadow::unbacked(0x400_0000, std::ptr::null_mut(), (2 * PIECE) as u64, true);
+    let first = shadow.span().0 as usize;
+    let ranges = [
+        CardRange {
+            shadow: shadow.clone(),
+            declared: (first + 16, first + 64),
+            span: shadow.card_span(first + 16, first + 64).unwrap(),
+            at: 17,
+            compared: true,
+            picked: false,
+        },
+        CardRange {
+            shadow: shadow.clone(),
+            declared: (first + 128, first + PIECE + 32),
+            span: shadow.card_span(first + 128, first + PIECE + 32).unwrap(),
+            at: 17,
+            compared: true,
+            picked: false,
+        },
+    ];
+    let base = slot.payload.1;
+    let meta = slot.meta.1 as *mut u32;
+    let capture = Capture {
+        base: 17,
+        count: 2,
+        slot,
+    };
+    let mut pieces = [
+        (shadow.clone(), 16, 64, 0),
+        (shadow.clone(), 128, PIECE, 48),
+        (Arc::clone(&shadow), PIECE, PIECE + 32, PIECE as u64 - 80),
+    ];
+    let previous = pieces.each_ref().map(|p| p.3);
+    // SAFETY: this fixture owns the inactive slot and emulates fenced GPU results.
+    unsafe {
+        meta.add(4).write(1);
+        meta.add(5).write(u32::MAX);
+        assert_eq!(capture.map(&ranges, &mut pieces), None);
+        assert_eq!(pieces.each_ref().map(|p| p.3), previous);
+        meta.add(5).write(4);
+        assert_eq!(capture.map(&ranges, &mut pieces), Some(base));
+        assert_eq!(
+            pieces.each_ref().map(|p| p.3),
+            [
+                (PIECE + 16) as u64,
+                (PIECE + 128) as u64,
+                (4 * PIECE) as u64
+            ]
+        );
+    }
+    eprintln!("capture publishes all offsets together or keeps the original fetch");
+}

diff --git a/crates/victory-gpu/src/launch.hlsl b/crates/victory-gpu/src/launch.hlsl

@@ -0,0 +1,64 @@
+// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-or-later
+// Adapted from fxceless/victory-fork b1d777a, after wildheathen #1381
+// and stellarr-studio/kelvaroq native patch 0041. See launch_code.rs.
+
+struct Payload {
+    uint unused;
+};
+groupshared Payload payload;
+
+cbuffer Launch : register(b0, space9) {
+    uint take_rest;
+};
+ByteAddressBuffer record : register(t0, space9);
+RWByteAddressBuffer budget : register(u0, space9);
+
+static const uint BUDGET = 1u << 27;
+
+[numthreads(1, 1, 1)]
+void main() {
+    uint3 groups = record.Load3(0);
+    // In this renderer an oversized X is an unwritten argument slot.
+    if (groups.x > 65535u) {
+        groups = uint3(0, 0, 0);
+    }
+    groups.yz = min(groups.yz, uint2(1, 1));
+    uint cost = groups.x * groups.y * groups.z * 128u;
+
+    uint ignored;
+    if (groups.x != 0) {
+        budget.InterlockedMax(4, groups.x, ignored);
+        budget.InterlockedAdd(8, groups.x, ignored);
+    }
+    if (cost >= (1u << 22)) {
+        budget.InterlockedMax(16, cost, ignored);
+    }
+
+    if (cost != 0) {
+        uint before;
+        budget.InterlockedOr(0, 0, before);
+        bool fit = false;
+        [allow_uav_condition]
+        while (before < BUDGET) {
+            bool fits_now = cost <= BUDGET - before;
+            if (!fits_now && take_rest == 0) {
+                break;
+            }
+            uint next = fits_now ? before + cost : BUDGET;
+            uint previous;
+            budget.InterlockedCompareExchange(0, before, next, previous);
+            if (previous == before) {
+                fit = fits_now;
+                break;
+            }
+            before = previous;
+        }
+        if (!fit) {
+            groups = uint3(0, 0, 0);
+            budget.InterlockedAdd(12, 1, ignored);
+        }
+    }
+
+    payload.unused = 0;
+    DispatchMesh(groups.x, groups.y, groups.z, payload);
+}

+//! The ordered stand-alone mesh clamp inside an amplification shader.
+
+use std::sync::OnceLock;
+use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicU64, Ordering};
+use windows::Win32::Graphics::Direct3D12::*;
+
+#[path = "launch_code.rs"]
+mod code;
+
+static LAUNCHED: AtomicU64 = AtomicU64::new(0);
+static FALLBACK: AtomicU64 = AtomicU64::new(0);
+
+pub(crate) fn on() -> bool {
+    static ON: OnceLock<bool> = OnceLock::new();
+    *ON.get_or_init(|| std::env::var_os("VICTORY_MESH_LAUNCH").is_some_and(|value| value == "1"))
+}
+
+pub(crate) fn shader() -> Option<&'static [u8]> {
+    code::shader()
+}
+
+pub(crate) fn bytecode(bytes: &[u8]) -> D3D12_SHADER_BYTECODE {
+    D3D12_SHADER_BYTECODE {
+        pShaderBytecode: bytes.as_ptr().cast(),
+        BytecodeLength: bytes.len(),
+    }
+}
+
+// Keep the existing root parameter indices. Three appended parameters cost five dwords.
+pub(crate) fn params() -> [D3D12_ROOT_PARAMETER1; 3] {
+    const SPACE: u32 = 9; // launch.hlsl
+    let descriptor = |kind| D3D12_ROOT_PARAMETER1 {
+        ParameterType: kind,
+        Anonymous: D3D12_ROOT_PARAMETER1_0 {
+            Descriptor: D3D12_ROOT_DESCRIPTOR1 {
+                ShaderRegister: 0,
+                RegisterSpace: SPACE,
+                Flags: D3D12_ROOT_DESCRIPTOR_FLAG_DATA_VOLATILE,
+            },
+        },
+        ShaderVisibility: D3D12_SHADER_VISIBILITY_AMPLIFICATION,
+    };
+    [
+        D3D12_ROOT_PARAMETER1 {
+            ParameterType: D3D12_ROOT_PARAMETER_TYPE_32BIT_CONSTANTS,
+            Anonymous: D3D12_ROOT_PARAMETER1_0 {
+                Constants: D3D12_ROOT_CONSTANTS {
+                    ShaderRegister: 0,
+                    RegisterSpace: SPACE,
+                    Num32BitValues: 1,
+                },
+            },
+            ShaderVisibility: D3D12_SHADER_VISIBILITY_AMPLIFICATION,
+        },
+        descriptor(D3D12_ROOT_PARAMETER_TYPE_SRV),
+        descriptor(D3D12_ROOT_PARAMETER_TYPE_UAV),
+    ]
+}
+
+/// Records the draw after the same producer wait as the standalone clamp.
+/// The caller has bound the launch pipeline and pinned the argument buffer.
+pub(crate) fn record(
+    list: &ID3D12GraphicsCommandList6,
+    first: u32,
+    record: u64,
+    budget: u64,
+    take_rest: u32,
+) {
+    // SAFETY: open list with this root signature and live argument/budget buffers.
+    unsafe {
+        list.ResourceBarrier(&[crate::indirect::uav_barrier()]);
+        list.SetGraphicsRoot32BitConstant(first, take_rest, 0);
+        list.SetGraphicsRootShaderResourceView(first + 1, record);
+        list.SetGraphicsRootUnorderedAccessView(first + 2, budget);
+        list.DispatchMesh(1, 1, 1);
+    }
+}
+
+pub(crate) fn count(launched: bool) {
+    let counter = if launched { &LAUNCHED } else { &FALLBACK };
+    counter.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
+}
+
+pub(crate) fn line(frames: f64) {
+    if !on() {
+        return;
+    }
+    let launched = LAUNCHED.swap(0, Ordering::Relaxed) as f64 / frames;
+    let fallback = FALLBACK.swap(0, Ordering::Relaxed) as f64 / frames;
+    tracing::info!(
+        "ordered mesh launch per frame: {launched:.1} launched, {fallback:.1} fell back to the clamp"
+    );
+}

+//! The AS is compiled from our launch.hlsl. Its provenance is fxceless
+//! b1d777a8071a6104e4266162d3ac4cfef9818fec, after wildheathen #1381 and
+//! stellarr-studio/kelvaroq native patch 0041. The local HLSL preserves the
+//! receiver's empty records and both budget policies.
+//!
+//! For an embedded AS, set VICTORY_MESH_LAUNCH_DXC to a host-runnable dxc
+//! executable (or wrapper) at build time. The build runs:
+//! dxc -nologo -T as_6_6 -E main -Fo launch.dxil launch.hlsl
+//! Tested with DXC 1.9.2609.5 (01b62ad47) and its matching dxil.dll:
+//! HLSL SHA-256 e68299d4a7f78c97bd9142e919045531e07b2598011dbb52082e626421614088
+//! DXIL SHA-256 599ebb94610a12a0d0b088c734eaa8f47b81fca2686850d844972cbada9cc222
+
+static EMBEDDED: &[u8] = include_bytes!(concat!(env!("OUT_DIR"), "/launch.dxil"));
+
+pub(super) fn shader() -> Option<&'static [u8]> {
+    if !EMBEDDED.is_empty() {
+        return Some(EMBEDDED);
+    }
+
+    static COMPILED: std::sync::OnceLock<Option<Vec<u8>>> = std::sync::OnceLock::new();
+    COMPILED.get_or_init(|| {
+        crate::dxc::compile(include_str!("launch.hlsl"), "main", "as_6_6")
+            .inspect_err(|error| tracing::info!(%error, "mesh launch compiler unavailable, using plain pipelines"))
+            .ok()
+    }).as_deref()
+}

+    #[test]
+    #[ignore = "requires a mesh-capable D3D12 device and PC DXC"]
+    fn ordered_mesh_launch_matches_the_plain_clamp() {
+        use windows::core::Interface;
+
+        const RECORDS: usize = 6;
+        const PRODUCER: &str = r#"
+            RWByteAddressBuffer args : register(u0);
+            RWByteAddressBuffer budget : register(u1);
+            cbuffer Seed : register(b0) { uint spent; };
+            [RootSignature("UAV(u0), UAV(u1), RootConstants(num32BitConstants=1, b0)")]
+            [numthreads(1, 1, 1)]
+            void main() {
+                args.Store3(0, uint3(~0u, ~0u, ~0u));
+                args.Store3(16, uint3(3, 2, 9));
+                args.Store3(32, uint3(2, 0, 1));
+                args.Store3(48, uint3(4, 1, 1));
+                args.Store3(64, uint3(3, 1, 1));
+                args.Store3(80, uint3(2, 1, 1));
+                budget.Store(0, spent);
+            }

+        const MESH: &str = r#"
+            struct V { float4 p : SV_Position; };
+            struct P { uint unused; };
+            RWByteAddressBuffer result : register(u0);
+            [outputtopology("triangle")] [numthreads(1, 1, 1)]
+            void main(in payload P pl, uint3 id : SV_GroupID,
+                      out vertices V v[3], out indices uint3 t[1]) {
+                SetMeshOutputCounts(3, 1);
+                v[0].p = v[1].p = v[2].p = float4(0, 0, 0, 1);
+                t[0] = uint3(0, 1, 2);
+                uint was;
+                result.InterlockedAdd(0, 1, was);
+                result.InterlockedOr(4, 1u << min(id.x, 31u), was);
+            }
+        "#;
+
+        let host = host().expect("D3D12 host for mesh launch");
+        assert!(host.mesh_shaders(), "SM 6.6 mesh support required");
+        let dxc = dxc().expect("PC DXC for synthetic shaders");
+        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
+        let cs = compile(&dxc, dir.path(), PRODUCER, "cs_6_6");
+        let ms = compile(&dxc, dir.path(), MESH, "ms_6_6");
+        let plain_ms = compile(&dxc, dir.path(), &MESH.replace("in payload P pl, ", ""), "ms_6_6");
+        let no_ps = D3D12_SHADER_BYTECODE::default();
+        // SAFETY: the synthetic compute program contains its root signature.
+        let producer_root: ID3D12RootSignature = unsafe { host.device.CreateRootSignature(0, &cs).unwrap() };
+        let producer = unsafe {
+            host.device.CreateComputePipelineState::<ID3D12PipelineState>(&D3D12_COMPUTE_PIPELINE_STATE_DESC {
+                pRootSignature: std::mem::transmute_copy(&producer_root),
+                CS: code(&cs),
+                ..Default::default()
+            }).unwrap()
+        };
+        let output = D3D12_ROOT_PARAMETER1 {
+            ParameterType: D3D12_ROOT_PARAMETER_TYPE_UAV,
+            Anonymous: D3D12_ROOT_PARAMETER1_0 {
+                Descriptor: D3D12_ROOT_DESCRIPTOR1 {
+                    ShaderRegister: 0,
+                    RegisterSpace: 0,
+                    Flags: D3D12_ROOT_DESCRIPTOR_FLAG_DATA_VOLATILE,
+                },
+            },
+            ShaderVisibility: D3D12_SHADER_VISIBILITY_MESH,
+        };
+        let (plain_root, _) = root_signature(&host.device, &[output], &[], false).unwrap();
+        let mut params = vec![output];
+        params.extend(crate::launch::params());
+        let (launch_root, _) = root_signature(&host.device, &params, &[], false).unwrap();
+        let mut state = GraphicsState::default();
+        state.depth.DepthEnable = false.into();
+        let plain = mesh_pipeline(
+            &host.device, &plain_root, (None, code(&plain_ms), no_ps), &state, false, "mesh launch fixture plain",
+        ).expect("zero-payload fallback");
+        let as_bytes = crate::launch::shader().expect("embedded AS or runtime DXC");
+        let launch = mesh_pipeline(
+            &host.device, &launch_root, (Some(crate::launch::bytecode(as_bytes)), code(&ms), no_ps),
+            &state, false, "mesh launch fixture AS",
+        ).expect("embedded amplification stage");
+        let indirect = crate::draw::host_signature(10, 16).unwrap();
+        let heaps = [
+            host.fallback_view_heap().cloned(),
+            host.fallback_sampler_heap().cloned(),
+        ];
+        assert!(heaps.iter().all(Option::is_some));
+        let rest = crate::indirect::rest();
+
+        let run = |launch_mask: u32, spent: u32| {
+            let list = host.new_list().unwrap();
+            let l6 = list.list.cast::<ID3D12GraphicsCommandList6>().unwrap();
+            let budget = list.budget().unwrap();
+            let args = host.uav_buffer(256).unwrap();
+            let (output, cpu) = host.shared_buffer(256).unwrap();
+            // SAFETY: both buffers and the shared result mapping outlive the
+            // waited submission. The producer and consumers use the renderer's
+            // buffer convention and order first through the production wait.
+            unsafe {
+                let args_gpu = args.GetGPUVirtualAddress();
+                let output_gpu = output.GetGPUVirtualAddress();
+                cpu.write_bytes(0, RECORDS * 8);
+                list.list.SetComputeRootSignature(&producer_root);
+                list.list.SetPipelineState(&producer);
+                list.list.SetComputeRootUnorderedAccessView(0, args_gpu);
+                list.list.SetComputeRootUnorderedAccessView(1, budget);
+                list.list.SetComputeRoot32BitConstant(2, spent, 0);
+                list.list.Dispatch(1, 1, 1);
+                list.list.SetDescriptorHeaps(&heaps);
+                for index in 0..RECORDS {
+                    let offset = index as u64 * 16;
+                    let result = output_gpu + index as u64 * 8;
+                    if launch_mask & (1 << index) != 0 {
+                        list.list.SetGraphicsRootSignature(&launch_root);
+                        list.list.SetPipelineState(&launch);
+                        list.list.SetGraphicsRootUnorderedAccessView(0, result);
+                        crate::launch::record(&l6, 1, args_gpu + offset, budget, rest);
+                    } else {
+                        let bounded = crate::indirect::bound(&list, (&args, offset), None, (10, 16, 1)).unwrap();
+                        list.list.SetGraphicsRootSignature(&plain_root);
+                        list.list.SetPipelineState(&plain);
+                        list.list.SetGraphicsRootUnorderedAccessView(0, result);
+                        list.list.ExecuteIndirect(&indirect, 1, &bounded, 0, None::<&ID3D12Resource>, 0);
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+            list.close();
+            host.execute(&[&list]);
+            // SAFETY: the host finished all commands that write the shared buffer.
+            std::array::from_fn::<_, RECORDS, _>(|at| unsafe {
+                let words = cpu.cast::<u32>().add(at * 2);
+                (words.read_volatile(), words.add(1).read_volatile())
+            })
+        };
+
+        for (spent, expected) in [
+            (0, [(0, 0), (3, 7), (0, 0), (4, 15), (3, 7), (2, 3)]),
+            (
+                (1 << 27) - 6 * 128,
+                [(0, 0), (3, 7), (0, 0), (0, 0), if rest == 0 { (3, 7) } else { (0, 0) }, (0, 0)],
+            ),
+        ] {
+            for launch_mask in [0, 0b111111, 0b101011, 0b010100] {
+                assert_eq!(run(launch_mask, spent), expected, "launch mask {launch_mask:06b}, spent {spent}");
+            }
+        }
+    }

+/// Cold-only wait on the first request for each pipeline. Later requests
+/// still share the normal per-frame budget.
+fn first_wait_budget() -> Duration {
+    static BUDGET: OnceLock<Duration> = OnceLock::new();
+    *BUDGET.get_or_init(|| {
+        let millis = std::env::var("VICTORY_PIPELINE_WAIT_FIRST_MS")
+            .ok()
+            .and_then(|value| value.trim().parse::<u64>().ok())
+            .unwrap_or(0);
+        if millis != 0 {
+            tracing::info!(millis, "host pipeline wait budget for a first use");
+        }
+        Duration::from_millis(millis)
+    })
+}

+fn wait_for_pipeline(p: &Pending, budget: Duration) -> Duration {
+    let waiting = crate::timeline::span("pipeline: first use waits for a host pipeline");
+    let started = Instant::now();
+    let (lock, done) = &DONE;
+    let mut held = lock.lock().unwrap_or_else(|e| e.into_inner());
+    while p.made.get().is_none() {
+        let rest = budget.saturating_sub(started.elapsed());
+        if rest.is_zero() {
+            break;
+        }
+        held = done.wait_timeout(held, rest).unwrap_or_else(|e| e.into_inner()).0;
+    }
+    drop(held);
+    let elapsed = started.elapsed();
+    waiting.end();
     #[cfg(feature = "diagnostics")]

-    );
+    crate::copystat::add(crate::copystat::C::PipelineWaitUs, elapsed.as_micros() as u64);
+    elapsed

+                done.notify_all();
+            });

+/// Gives each complete descriptor to one `descriptor::sync_heaps` part,
+/// from the sorted, disjoint mark runs `(first, count)` and the fallback
+/// `pages`, trimming or dropping any marked run that overlaps a fallback page.
+/// Also returns the sorted, disjoint slot runs `(first, count)` that were
+/// taken by [`take`] and fall inside a fallback page so [`missed`] does not
+/// count them as unreported.
+pub(crate) fn plan_parts(
+    start: u64,
+    end: u64,
+    step: u64,
+    mirrored_count: u32,
+    runs: &[(u32, u32)],
+    pages: &[usize],
+    page_size: usize,
+) -> (Vec<(usize, usize, bool)>, Vec<(u32, u32)>) {
+    let start_usize = start as usize;
+    let end_usize = end as usize;
+    let step_usize = step as usize;
+    let mirrored = u64::from(mirrored_count);
+
+    let mut check_parts: Vec<(usize, usize)> = Vec::with_capacity(pages.len());
+    let mut check_slots: Vec<(u64, u64)> = Vec::with_capacity(pages.len());
+    for &page in pages {
+        let p_from = page.max(start_usize);
+        let p_to = page.saturating_add(page_size).min(end_usize);
+        if p_from >= p_to {
+            continue;
+        }
+        check_parts.push((p_from, p_to));
+        let c_first = ((p_from - start_usize) as u64).div_ceil(step);
+        let c_end = ((p_to - start_usize) as u64 / step).min(mirrored);
+        if c_first < c_end {
+            check_slots.push((c_first, c_end));
+        }
+    }
+    check_slots.sort_unstable_by_key(|&(first, _)| first);
+    let mut merged_checks: Vec<(u64, u64)> = Vec::with_capacity(check_slots.len());
+    for (c_first, c_end) in check_slots {
+        match merged_checks.last_mut() {
+            Some((_, prev_end)) if c_first <= *prev_end => {
+                *prev_end = (*prev_end).max(c_end);
+            }
+            _ => merged_checks.push((c_first, c_end)),
+        }
+    }
+
+    let mut parts: Vec<(usize, usize, bool)> = Vec::with_capacity(runs.len() + check_parts.len());
+    let mut taken_in_check: Vec<(u32, u32)> = Vec::new();
+    let mut ci = 0usize;
+    for &(first, n) in runs {
+        if n == 0 {
+            continue;
+        }
+        let mut cur = u64::from(first);
+        let r_end = cur + u64::from(n);
+        while ci < merged_checks.len() && merged_checks[ci].1 <= cur {
+            ci += 1;
+        }
+        while ci < merged_checks.len() && merged_checks[ci].0 < r_end {
+            let (c_first, c_end) = merged_checks[ci];
+            if cur < c_first {
+                let len = c_first - cur;
+                let from = start_usize + cur as usize * step_usize;
+                parts.push((from, from + len as usize * step_usize, false));
+            }
+            let ov_start = cur.max(c_first);
+            let ov_end = r_end.min(c_end);
+            if ov_start < ov_end {
+                let ov_first = ov_start as u32;
+                let ov_len = (ov_end - ov_start) as u32;
+                match taken_in_check.last_mut() {
+                    Some((prev_first, prev_len)) if u64::from(*prev_first) + u64::from(*prev_len) == ov_start => {
+                        *prev_len += ov_len;
+                    }
+                    _ => taken_in_check.push((ov_first, ov_len)),
+                }
+            }
+            cur = ov_end;
+            if c_end <= r_end {
+                ci += 1;
+            } else {
+                break;
+            }
+        }
+        if cur < r_end {
+            let len = r_end - cur;
+            let from = start_usize + cur as usize * step_usize;
+            parts.push((from, from + len as usize * step_usize, false));
+        }
+    }
+    for (p_from, p_to) in check_parts {
+        parts.push((p_from, p_to, true));
+    }
+    (parts, taken_in_check)
+}
+
+/// Whether `index` lies in `taken_in_check` (sorted, disjoint `(first, count)` runs).
+pub(crate) fn was_taken_in_check(taken_in_check: &[(u32, u32)], index: u32) -> bool {
+    let i = taken_in_check.partition_point(|&(first, _)| first <= index);
+    i > 0 && {
+        let (first, count) = taken_in_check[i - 1];
+        u64::from(index) < u64::from(first) + u64::from(count)
+    }
+}
+

+/// since the start should be logged.
+#[inline]
+fn should_warn_missed(n: u64) -> bool {
+    n < 20 || n.is_power_of_two()
+}

+    #[test]
+    fn plan_parts_keeps_marked_runs_and_check_pages_disjoint() {
+        let start = 0x2000_0000u64;
+        let step = 32u64;
+        let page_size = 65536usize; // 2048 slots per page
+        let count = 8192u32;
+        let end = start + u64::from(count) * step;
+        let start_usize = start as usize;
+
+        // Pass fallback pages in wrap-around order (page 2, then page 1).
+        let pages = [start_usize + 2 * page_size, start_usize + page_size];
+        let runs = [
+            (100, 10),   // wholly before page 1
+            (2040, 15),  // straddles start of page 1 (2040..2048 kept, 2048..2055 in page 1)
+            (2060, 10),  // wholly inside page 1
+            (4090, 12),  // straddles page 1 / page 2 boundary (4090..4102 wholly inside merged checks)
+            (6140, 10),  // straddles end of page 2 (6140..6144 in page 2, 6144..6150 kept)
+        ];
+
+        let (parts, taken_in_check) = plan_parts(start, end, step, count, &runs, &pages, page_size);
+
+        let false_parts: Vec<(usize, usize)> = parts.iter().filter(|&&(_, _, check)| !check).map(|&(f, t, _)| (f, t)).collect();
+        let true_parts: Vec<(usize, usize)> = parts.iter().filter(|&&(_, _, check)| check).map(|&(f, t, _)| (f, t)).collect();
+
+        assert_eq!(
+            false_parts,
+            vec![
+                (start_usize + 100 * 32, start_usize + 110 * 32),
+                (start_usize + 2040 * 32, start_usize + 2048 * 32),
+                (start_usize + 6144 * 32, start_usize + 6150 * 32),
+            ]
+        );
+        assert_eq!(
+            true_parts,
+            vec![
+                (start_usize + 2 * page_size, start_usize + 3 * page_size),
+                (start_usize + page_size, start_usize + 2 * page_size),
+            ]
+        );
+
+        // Verify all parts are pairwise disjoint.
+        let mut all_ranges: Vec<(usize, usize)> = parts.iter().map(|&(f, t, _)| (f, t)).collect();
+        all_ranges.sort_unstable();
+        for w in all_ranges.windows(2) {
+            assert!(w[0].1 <= w[1].0, "overlapping parts: {:?} and {:?}", w[0], w[1]);
+        }
+
+        // Verify taken_in_check covers exact overlapping slots.
+        assert_eq!(taken_in_check, vec![(2048, 7), (2060, 10), (4090, 12), (6140, 4)]);
+        assert!(!was_taken_in_check(&taken_in_check, 2047));
+        assert!(was_taken_in_check(&taken_in_check, 2048));
+        assert!(was_taken_in_check(&taken_in_check, 2054));
+        assert!(!was_taken_in_check(&taken_in_check, 2055));
+        assert!(was_taken_in_check(&taken_in_check, 4096));
+        assert!(was_taken_in_check(&taken_in_check, 6143));
+        assert!(!was_taken_in_check(&taken_in_check, 6144));
+    }

+    fn missed_counts_tabled_after_marked_and_keeps_global_warn_counter() {
+        let _guard = SERIAL.lock().unwrap_or_else(|e| e.into_inner());
+        let start = 0x3000_0000u64;
+        assert!(adopt_now(start, 256));
+        for c in &CHECKED {
+            c.store(0, Relaxed);
+        }
+        MISSED_ALL.store(0, Relaxed);
+        WRITERS.lock().unwrap().clear();
+
+        // Slot 5 is marked: pending (CHECKED[3]) increments, tabled (CHECKED[5]) and missed (CHECKED[1], MISSED_ALL) do not.
+        mark(start + 5 * 32);
+        missed(5, start + 5 * 32, true, true);
+        assert_eq!(CHECKED[3].load(Relaxed), 1);
+        assert_eq!(CHECKED[1].load(Relaxed), 0);
+        assert_eq!(CHECKED[5].load(Relaxed), 0);
+        assert_eq!(MISSED_ALL.load(Relaxed), 0);
+
+        // Unmarked tabled view record increments both missed and tabled, plus MISSED_ALL.
+        missed(6, start + 6 * 32, true, true);
+        assert_eq!(CHECKED[1].load(Relaxed), 1);
+        assert_eq!(CHECKED[5].load(Relaxed), 1);
+        assert_eq!(MISSED_ALL.load(Relaxed), 1);
+
+        // Even after line() resets CHECKED[1], the global warn sequence continues past 20 without resetting.
+        for i in 7..28 {
+            missed(i, start + u64::from(i) * 32, true, false);
+        }
+        CHECKED[1].store(0, Relaxed);
+        let n_before = MISSED_ALL.load(Relaxed);
+        assert_eq!(n_before, 22);
+        assert!(!should_warn_missed(n_before));
+        missed(28, start + 28 * 32, true, false);
+        assert_eq!(CHECKED[1].load(Relaxed), 1);
+        assert_eq!(MISSED_ALL.load(Relaxed), 23);
+        assert!(should_warn_missed(32));
+        assert!(!should_warn_missed(33));
+
+        let_go(start);
+        WRITERS.lock().unwrap().clear();
+    }
+

+        }

+    fn small_overlap_unchanged(
+        &self,
+        busy: &[(usize, usize)],
+        pending: &std::sync::MutexGuard<'_, Vec<(usize, usize)>>,
+    ) -> bool {
+        let mut scratch = [0; 64];
+        busy.iter().fold(0usize, |n, &(a, e)| n.saturating_add(e - a)) <= scratch.len()
+            && self.compare_unwatched(busy, pending, &mut scratch) == Some(true)
+    }
+
+    // shortcut: cap a comparison at 32 MiB and settle if equality cannot be proved.
+    fn wide_overlap_unchanged(
+        &self,
+        busy: Vec<(usize, usize)>,
+        pending: &std::sync::MutexGuard<'_, Vec<(usize, usize)>>,
+    ) -> bool {
+        const CAP: usize = 32 << 20;
+        let size = busy.iter().fold(0usize, |n, &(a, e)| n.saturating_add(e - a));
+        if size <= 64 {
+            return false; // the separate small comparison already tried these bytes
+        }
+        LARGE_OVERLAPS[4].fetch_max(size as u64, Ordering::Relaxed);
+        if size > CAP {
+            LARGE_OVERLAPS[3].fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
+            return false;
+        }
+        thread_local! {
+            static SCRATCH: std::cell::RefCell<Box<[u8]>> = std::cell::RefCell::new(vec![0; 64 << 10].into_boxed_slice());
+        }
+        let started = std::time::Instant::now();
+        let answer = SCRATCH.with_borrow_mut(|scratch| self.compare_unwatched(&busy, pending, scratch));
+        LARGE_OVERLAPS[5].fetch_add(started.elapsed().as_nanos() as u64, Ordering::Relaxed);
+        let stat = match answer {
+            Some(true) => 0,
+            Some(false) => 1,
+            None => 2,
+        };
+        LARGE_OVERLAPS[stat].fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
+        answer == Some(true)
+    }
+
+    /// `None` means there was no stable, readable reference for the comparison.
+    fn compare_unwatched(
+        &self,
+        busy: &[(usize, usize)],
+        _pending: &std::sync::MutexGuard<'_, Vec<(usize, usize)>>,
+        scratch: &mut [u8],
+    ) -> Option<bool> {
+        if scratch.is_empty() {
+            return None;
+        }
+        // A landing may update both the reference and the title bytes.
+        // Read both while that merge is excluded, as in the watched path.
+        let Ok(_merging) = self.merging.try_write() else {
+            return None;
+        };
+        let Ok(before) = self.before.try_lock() else {
+            return None;
+        };
+        let held = if self.card.is_some() {
+            self.data.cast_const()
+        } else if let Some(snapshot) = before.as_ref() {
+            snapshot.as_ptr()
+        } else {
+            return None;
+        };
+        for &(from, to) in busy {
+            let mut at = from;
+            while at < to {
+                let len = scratch.len().min(to - at);
+                let title = &mut scratch[..len];
+                if !crate::host::read_into(at as *const u8, title) {
+                    return None;
+                }
+                // SAFETY: all chunks lie inside a pending range whose bytes
+                // were snapshotted before the flight. The pending and merge
+                // locks keep the snapshot alive and exclude its writers.
+                let reference = unsafe { std::slice::from_raw_parts(held.add(at - self.start as usize), len) };
+                if title != reference {
+                    return Some(false);
+                }
+                at += len;
+            }
+        }
+        Some(true)
+    }
+
     /// Of [`Shadow::merge_under_writes`]: title memory `[lo, lo + n)`, in one
     /// page, compared with the reference, which begins at `held`, and put in.
     /// The runs that changed, none if it cannot be read, and an error if
@@ -4692,6 +4829,75 @@ impl Shadow {

+#[test]
+fn small_overlap_proves_unchanged_title_bytes_on_each_reference_path() {
+    let mut title = [11u8; 128];
+    let mut mirror = [11u8; 128];
+    let at = title.as_mut_ptr() as usize;
+    for card in [false, true] {
+        let copy = Shadow::unbacked(at as u64, mirror.as_mut_ptr(), 128, card);
+        let mut pending = copy.pending.lock().unwrap();
+        pending.push((at, at + 128));
+        let eight = [(at + 4, at + 12)];
+        if !card {
+            assert!(!copy.small_overlap_unchanged(&eight, &pending));
+            *copy.before.lock().unwrap() = Some(mirror.to_vec());
+        }
+        assert!(copy.small_overlap_unchanged(&eight, &pending));
+        assert!(copy.small_overlap_unchanged(&[(at, at + 4), (at + 8, at + 12)], &pending));
+        assert!(!copy.small_overlap_unchanged(&[(at, at + 65)], &pending));
+        title[5] = 22;
+        assert!(!copy.small_overlap_unchanged(&eight, &pending));
+        assert_eq!(mirror[5], 11);
+        title[5] = 11;
+    }

+    pending.push((at, at + LEN));
+    let ranges = [(at, at + LEN)];

+    assert_eq!(copy.compare_unwatched(&ranges, &pending, &mut scratch), Some(true));
+    title[LEN - 1] = 29;
+    assert_eq!(copy.compare_unwatched(&ranges, &pending, &mut scratch), Some(false));
+    assert_eq!(reference[LEN - 1], 31);
+    let _landing = copy.merging.read().unwrap();

+    #[cfg(feature = "diagnostics")]

+        assert_eq!(copy.pending_overlap(at + LEN, at + LEN + 8), None);

+    let mut bytes = before.clone();
+    let changed = [0, SKEW - 1, SKEW, (15 << 20) + 17, (23 << 20) + 2, LEN - 1];
+    for at in changed {
+        bytes[at] = 1;
+    }
+    let shadow = Shadow::unbacked((PIECE + SKEW) as u64, bytes.as_mut_ptr(), LEN as u64, false);
+    let found = shadow.differing(0, LEN, &before);
+    for at in changed {
+        assert!(found.iter().any(|&(offset, len)| (offset..offset + len).contains(&at)), "missed byte {at:#x}");
+    }
+    for (offset, len) in found {
+        assert!(changed.iter().any(|&at| (offset..offset + len).contains(&at)));
+    }

+pub(crate) fn asked_stale(start: usize, end: usize, base: usize, copied: &[u64]) -> Option<bool> {

+fn stale_in_notes(notes: &Notes, first: usize, last: usize, base: usize, copied: &[u64]) -> bool {
+    let from = first.saturating_sub(base);
+    let to = last.saturating_sub(base).min(copied.len());
+    if from >= to {
+        return false;
+    }
+    let taken = &copied[from..to];
+    let start = from + base;
+    taken.contains(&0) || !notes.all(start, to + base, |page, write| write <= taken[page - start])
 }
