+//! Opt-in compatibility hooks for known title builds.
+//!
+//! Only per-build offsets and short byte signatures live here (asset policy:
+//! nothing of the title itself). Every hook checks the build's hash and the
+//! exact bytes it replaces, and does nothing otherwise.
+
+use windows_sys::Win32::System::Diagnostics::Debug::FlushInstructionCache;
+use windows_sys::Win32::System::Memory::{PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE, VirtualProtect};
+use windows_sys::Win32::System::Threading::GetCurrentProcess;
+
+/// First 16 hex digits of the BLAKE3 of the Series X 1.0.2.1 executable.
+const BUILD_1_0_2_1: &str = "b99f876b74d4a77f";
+
+/// Environment variable that turns the watchdog hook on.
+pub(crate) const NO_WATCHDOG_ENV: &str = "VICTORY_NO_WATCHDOG";
+
+/// One code patch: `rva` must hold exactly `expect` before `new` is written.
+struct Patch {
+    what: &'static str,
+    rva: usize,
+    expect: &'static [u8],
+    new: &'static [u8],
+}
+
+/// The title's watchdog thread ends the process on purpose when its main
+/// thread has not advanced for about 30 one-second ticks. Two places do it:
+/// the handler it calls (`mov dword ptr [0], imm32`) and, when the handler
+/// returns or is unset, a fallback right after the call (`mov dword ptr [0],
+/// 0; int3`). Jumping over the whole block returns the loop to its normal
+/// path; the first patch makes the handler harmless if something else calls it.
+const WATCHDOG: &[Patch] = &[
+    Patch {
+        what: "watchdog: skip the call and the fallback fault",
+        rva: 0x2215c27,
+        // mov rax, [rip + disp32] (the handler pointer)
+        expect: &[0x48, 0x8B, 0x05, 0x1A, 0x07, 0xFA, 0x04],
+        // jmp +0x18 (to the loop's normal continuation), then NOPs
+        new: &[0xEB, 0x18, 0x90, 0x90, 0x90, 0x90, 0x90],
+    },
+    Patch {
+        what: "watchdog: handler's deliberate fault",
+        rva: 0x23cf683,
+        expect: &[0xC7, 0x04, 0x25, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0x21, 0xD4, 0x8B, 0x4D],
+        // an eleven-byte NOP
+        new: &[0x66, 0x66, 0x2E, 0x0F, 0x1F, 0x84, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0],
+    },
+];
+
+/// Applies the watchdog patches whose bytes match. Returns how many applied.
+///
+/// # Safety
+/// `base` is the mapped image whose original BLAKE3 is `hash`, and no title
+/// code is running yet.
+pub(crate) unsafe fn disarm_watchdog(base: usize, hash: &blake3::Hash) -> usize {
+    if !hash.to_hex().starts_with(BUILD_1_0_2_1) {
+        return 0;
+    }
+    WATCHDOG
+        .iter()
+        // SAFETY: forwarded from this function's contract.
+        .filter(|p| unsafe { apply(base, p) })
+        .count()
+}
+
+/// # Safety
+/// See `disarm_watchdog`.
+unsafe fn apply(base: usize, p: &Patch) -> bool {
+    let at = (base + p.rva) as *mut u8;
+    // SAFETY: the offset is inside the mapped .text of this exact build.
+    let now = unsafe { std::slice::from_raw_parts(at, p.expect.len()) };
+    if now != p.expect || p.new.len() != p.expect.len() {
+        tracing::warn!(what = p.what, "hook skipped: the bytes are not the expected ones");
+        return false;
+    }
+    let mut old = 0;
+    // SAFETY: changing the protection of those bytes only, restored below.
+    if unsafe { VirtualProtect(at as _, p.new.len(), PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE, &mut old) } == 0 {
+        return false;
+    }
+    // SAFETY: writable now; the bytes are the ones checked above.
+    unsafe {
+        std::ptr::copy_nonoverlapping(p.new.as_ptr(), at, p.new.len());
+        VirtualProtect(at as _, p.new.len(), old, &mut old);
+        FlushInstructionCache(GetCurrentProcess(), at as _, p.new.len());
+    }
+    tracing::info!(what = p.what, "hook applied");
+    true
+}

+                            return None;
+                        }
+                        TypedHandle(kind, component)

+                    _ => return None,
+                })
+            })

+    }

+//! Fixed table accesses that move half bits through unsigned buffer views.
+//! Adapted from tippythetaidum's GPL-3.0-or-later PR927 and LinVxtory's
+//! preserved-lane analysis.
+
+use super::{
+    Fields, HashMap, HashSet, Inst, Lower, Operand, Reg, Role, RootType, Site, Source, Value,
+};
+
+impl Lower<'_> {
+    pub(super) fn table_place(&self, source: &Source) -> Option<(u8, u32)> {
+        let (Source::Loaded { load, first } | Source::Fetched { load, first }) = source else {
+            return None;
+        };
+        let address = self.scalar.get(load)?;
+        let parameter = self.x.native.layout.get(address.param)?;
+        (parameter.root == RootType::Table && address.dynamic.is_none())
+            .then(|| (parameter.slot, address.offset / 4 + u32::from(*first)))
+    }
+
+    /// A companion affects every access through its slot. A runtime selection
+    /// can alias any slot in its table.
+    pub(super) fn find_half_accesses(&self) -> HashMap<Site, (u8, u32)> {
+        let mut halves = HashMap::new();
+        let mut other = HashSet::new();
+        let mut picked = HashSet::new();
+        for descriptor in &self.x.res.descriptors {
+            if descriptor.role == Role::Sampler {
+                continue;
+            }
+            let (block, index) = descriptor.site;
+            if matches!(descriptor.source, Source::Loaded { .. })
+                && let Some(place) = self.table_place(&descriptor.source)
+                && self
+                    .x
+                    .p
+                    .blocks
+                    .get(block)
+                    .and_then(|block| block.insts.get(index))
+                    .is_some_and(|inst| self.moves_halves(inst))
+            {
+                halves.insert(descriptor.site, place);
+                continue;
+            }
+            for leaf in descriptor.source.leaves() {
+                if let Some(place) = self.table_place(leaf) {
+                    other.insert(place);
+                } else if let Source::Loaded { load, .. } | Source::Fetched { load, .. } = leaf
+                    && let Some(address) = self.scalar.get(load)
+                    && address.dynamic.is_some()
+                    && let Some(parameter) = self.x.native.layout.get(address.param)
+                    && parameter.root == RootType::Table
+                {
+                    picked.insert(parameter.slot);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        halves.retain(|_, place| !other.contains(place) && !picked.contains(&place.0));
+        halves
+    }
+
+    fn moves_halves(&self, inst: &Inst) -> bool {
+        let Fields::Buffer {
+            data: Some(Operand::Vector { reg, .. }),
+            format: None,
+            ..
+        } = inst.fields
+        else {
+            return false;
+        };
+        // Explicit-format tbuffer reads decode the raw data using their opcode format.
+        let name = inst.name();
+        if name.starts_with("buffer_load_format_d16_")
+            || name.starts_with("buffer_store_format_d16_")
+        {
+            return true;
+        }
+        let count = match name.strip_prefix("buffer_load_format_") {
+            Some("x") => 1,
+            Some("xy") => 2,
+            Some("xyz") => 3,
+            Some("xyzw") => 4,
+            _ => return false,
+        };
+        (0..count).all(|k| {
+            inst.write(Reg::V(reg + k))
+                .is_some_and(|value| self.read_only_as_halves(value))
+        })
+    }
+
+    /// Phis and masked writes can carry the original bits to later readers.
+    fn read_only_as_halves(&self, value: Value) -> bool {
+        let program = self.x.p;
+        let mut used = false;
+        let mut seen = HashSet::from([value]);
+        let mut todo = vec![value];
+        while let Some(value) = todo.pop() {
+            for block in &program.blocks {
+                for phi in block.phis.iter().filter(|phi| phi.args.contains(&value)) {
+                    if seen.insert(phi.value) {
+                        todo.push(phi.value);
+                    }
+                }
+                for inst in &block.insts {
+                    if !inst.reads.iter().any(|read| read.1 == value) {
+                        continue;
+                    }
+                    for &(reg, next) in &inst.writes {
+                        if matches!(reg, Reg::V(_))
+                            && inst.read(reg) == Some(value)
+                            && seen.insert(next)
+                        {
+                            todo.push(next);
+                        }
+                    }
+                    let operands = crate::ir::operand_reads(program, inst);
+                    let regs: Vec<_> = inst
+                        .reads
+                        .iter()
+                        .filter(|(reg, read)| *read == value && operands.contains(reg))
+                        .map(|&(reg, _)| reg)
+                        .collect();
+                    if regs.is_empty() {
+                        continue;
+                    }
+                    used = true;
+                    let Fields::Valu(valu) = &inst.fields else {
+                        return false;
+                    };
+                    match inst.name() {
+                        "v_cvt_f32_f16" => {}
+                        "v_fma_mix_f32" | "v_fma_mixlo_f16" | "v_fma_mixhi_f16" => {
+                            if valu.src.iter().enumerate().any(|(k, op)| {
+                                matches!(*op, Operand::Vector { reg, .. } if regs.contains(&Reg::V(reg)))
+                                    && valu.opsel_hi >> k & 1 == 0
+                            }) {
+                                return false;
+                            }
+                        }
+                        _ => return false,
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        used
+    }
+}
+

+//! CPU emitter regressions using synthetic GFX10 instructions.
+//! Adapted from the PR927 and LinVxtory half-view tests.
+
+use super::super::{Lowered, Parts, lower};
+use crate::emit::bitcode::HalfBufferCall::{self, *};
+use crate::native::{ComputeIo, RootParam, StageIo};
+use crate::{Container, HwStage, NativeProgram, ResourceType, RootType, UsedResource};
+
+const SAVE_EXEC: u32 = 0xBE94_037E;
+const ONLY_LANE_0: u32 = 0x7DA4_0080;
+const RESTORE_EXEC: u32 = 0xBEFE_0314;
+const SKIP_LOAD_AND_CONVERT: u32 = 0xBF88_0003;
+const LOAD_X: [u32; 2] = buffer(0, 1, 8);
+const LOAD_D16: [u32; 2] = buffer(0x83, 1, 8);
+const STORE_D16: [u32; 2] = buffer(0x87, 1, 12);
+
+const fn buffer(op: u32, data: u32, descriptor: u32) -> [u32; 2] {
+    [
+        0x38 << 26 | op << 18 | 1 << 13,
+        0x80 << 24 | (descriptor >> 2) << 16 | data << 8,
+    ]
+}
+
+const fn mov(dst: u32, src: u32) -> u32 {
+    0x7E00_0000 | dst << 17 | 1 << 9 | src
+}
+
+const fn cvt(dst: u32, src: u32) -> u32 {
+    0x7E00_0000 | dst << 17 | 0x0b << 9 | (256 + src)
+}
+
+const fn add(dst: u32, a: u32, b: u32) -> u32 {
+    0x03 << 25 | dst << 17 | b << 9 | (256 + a)
+}
+
+fn with_parts<T>(
+    body: &[u32],
+    root: RootType,
+    dynamic: bool,
+    f: impl FnOnce(&Parts<'_>) -> T,
+) -> T {
+    // s5 is the high half of the table base; v7 makes a size query observable.
+    let mut words = vec![0xBE85_0380, mov(7, 128)];
+    if dynamic {
+        words.push(0x7E2A_0500); // v_readfirstlane_b32 s21, v0
+    }
+    for (dst, offset) in [(8, 32), (12, 64), (16, 96)] {
+        words.extend([
+            0x3D << 26 | 2 << 18 | dst << 6 | 2,
+            if dynamic && dst == 8 {
+                21 << 25
+            } else {
+                125 << 25 | offset
+            },
+        ]);
+    }
+    words.extend_from_slice(body);
+    for (data, offset) in [(2, 0), (7, 4)] {
+        words.extend([
+            0x38 << 26 | 0x1C << 18 | offset,
+            0x80 << 24 | 4 << 16 | data << 8,
+        ]);
+    }
+    words.push(0xBF81_0000); // s_endpgm
+    let view = |kind, dword, stride| UsedResource {
+        kind,
+        root,
+        table_offset_dwords: dword,
+        lower_bound: dword / 8,
+        stride,
+        dwords: 4,
+        user_data: 4,
+        slot: 1,
+    };
+    let native = NativeProgram {
+        stage: HwStage::Cs,
+        wave64: false,
+        vgprs: 8,
+        layout: vec![
+            RootParam {
+                root: RootType::Constants,
+                table_offset_dwords: 0,
+                sgprs: 4,
+                base_register: 0,
+                user_data: 0,
+                slot: 0,
+            },
+            RootParam {
+                root,
+                table_offset_dwords: 0,
+                sgprs: 1,
+                base_register: 0,
+                user_data: 4,
+                slot: 1,
+            },
+        ],
+        used: if root == RootType::Table {
+            vec![
+                view(ResourceType::Srv, 0, 4),
+                view(ResourceType::Uav, 16, 4),
+                view(ResourceType::Uav, 24, 0),
+            ]
+        } else {
+            Vec::new()
+        },
+        io: StageIo::Cs(ComputeIo {
+            rsrc2: 5 << 1,
+            threads: [32, 1, 1],
+        }),
+        code: words.iter().flat_map(|w| w.to_le_bytes()).collect(),
+    };
+    let p = crate::ir::build(&native.code, false, 8).expect("IR");
+    let st = crate::structure::structure(&p).expect("structure");
+    let res = crate::resource::track(&p, &native.layout, native.stage, Some(&st))
+        .expect("resource tracking");
+    let io = crate::io::raise(&p, &native).expect("IO");
+    f(&Parts {
+        native: &native,
+        p: &p,
+        st: &st,
+        io: &io,
+        res: &res,
+        inputs: Vec::new(),
+        outputs: Vec::new(),
+        prims: Vec::new(),
+        mesh_stride: 0,
+    })
+}
+
+fn probe(body: &[u32], dynamic: bool) -> Lowered {
+    with_parts(body, RootType::Table, dynamic, |parts| {
+        lower(parts).expect("lower")
+    })
+}
+
+fn count(shader: &Lowered, call: HalfBufferCall) -> usize {
+    shader
+        .half_buffer_calls
+        .iter()
+        .filter(|&&c| c == call)
+        .count()
+}
+
+#[test]
+fn fixed_d16_moves_bits_and_names_exact_views() {
+    let body = [LOAD_D16.as_slice(), &STORE_D16, &[mov(7, 10)]].concat();
+    let shader = probe(&body, false);
+    assert_eq!(shader.half_views, [(1, 8), (1, 16)]);
+    assert_eq!(count(&shader, LoadUint), 1);
+    assert_eq!(count(&shader, StoreUint), 1);
+    for call in [LoadFloat, StoreFloat, FloatToHalf, HalfToFloat] {
+        assert_eq!(count(&shader, call), 0, "{call:?}");
+    }
+    assert!(count(&shader, Dimensions) > 0);
+    for kind in [10, 0x100a] {
+        assert!(count(&shader, TypedHandle(kind, 0x405)) > 0);
+        assert_eq!(count(&shader, TypedHandle(kind, 0x409)), 0);
+    }
+    with_parts(&body, RootType::Table, false, |p| {
+        let bytes = crate::dxbc::build(&[]);
+        let container = Container::parse(&bytes).expect("empty test container");
+        let (host, _) =
+            super::super::super::emit_program(&container, p.native, p.p, p.st, p.io, p.res)
+                .expect("emit host shader");
+        assert_eq!(host.half_views, shader.half_views);
+    });
+}
+
+#[test]
+fn ordinary_load_feeds_a_low_half_or_a_phi() {
+    // v_fma_mix_f32 v2, sel_lo(v1), 1.0, 0
+    let mix = [0xCC20_0002, (1 << 27) | (128 << 18) | (242 << 9) | 257];
+    let straight = [LOAD_X.as_slice(), &mix].concat();
+    let branched = [
+        &[mov(2, 128), SAVE_EXEC, ONLY_LANE_0, SKIP_LOAD_AND_CONVERT][..],
+        &LOAD_X,
+        &[
+            cvt(2, 1),
+            RESTORE_EXEC,
+            SAVE_EXEC,
+            ONLY_LANE_0,
+            mov(1, 128),
+            RESTORE_EXEC,
+        ],
+    ]
+    .concat();
+    for body in [straight, branched] {
+        let shader = probe(&body, false);
+        assert_eq!(shader.half_views, [(1, 8)]);
+        assert_eq!(count(&shader, LoadUint), 1);
+        assert_eq!(count(&shader, LoadFloat), 0);
+        assert!(count(&shader, HalfToFloat) > 0);
+    }
+}
+
+#[test]
+fn mixed_and_runtime_table_selection_keep_float_bindings() {
+    let mixed = [
+        LOAD_D16.as_slice(),
+        &buffer(0, 3, 8),
+        &[add(2, 3, 2)],
+        &STORE_D16,
+        &[mov(7, 10)],
+    ]
+    .concat();
+    let shader = probe(&mixed, false);
+    assert_eq!(shader.half_views, [(1, 16)]);
+    assert_eq!(count(&shader, LoadUint), 0);
+    assert_eq!(count(&shader, LoadFloat), 2);
+    assert_eq!(count(&shader, StoreUint), 1);
+    assert_eq!(count(&shader, TypedHandle(10, 0x405)), 0);
+    assert!(count(&shader, TypedHandle(10, 0x409)) > 0);
+
+    let shader = probe(&[LOAD_D16.as_slice(), &STORE_D16].concat(), true);
+    assert!(shader.half_views.is_empty());
+    assert_eq!(count(&shader, LoadFloat), 1);
+    assert_eq!(count(&shader, StoreFloat), 1);
+}
+
+#[test]
+fn a_masked_write_can_preserve_a_whole_float_use() {
+    for overwrite in [mov(1, 128), cvt(1, 1)] {
+        let body = [
+            LOAD_X.as_slice(),
+            &[
+                cvt(2, 1),
+                SAVE_EXEC,
+                ONLY_LANE_0,
+                overwrite,
+                RESTORE_EXEC,
+                add(2, 1, 2),
+            ],
+        ]
+        .concat();
+        let shader = probe(&body, false);
+        assert!(shader.half_views.is_empty());
+        assert_eq!(count(&shader, LoadFloat), 1);
+        assert_eq!(count(&shader, LoadUint), 0);
+    }
+}
+
+#[test]
+fn heap_indexed_and_explicit_format_d16_keep_their_lowering() {
+    let heap = with_parts(
+        &[LOAD_D16.as_slice(), &STORE_D16].concat(),
+        RootType::ResourceHeap,
+        false,
+        |parts| lower(parts).expect("heap lowering"),
+    );
+    assert!(heap.half_views.is_empty());
+    assert_eq!(count(&heap, LoadFloat), 1);
+    assert_eq!(count(&heap, StoreFloat), 1);
+    assert!(heap.root_srvs.contains(&crate::emit::SLOT_STRIDES));
+
+    // tbuffer_load_format_d16_xy v2, off, s[8:11], 0 format:16_16_UINT
+    let explicit = probe(&[0xE8D9_0000, 0x8022_0200], false);
+    assert!(explicit.half_views.is_empty());
+    assert!(count(&explicit, RawLoad) > 0);
+    assert_eq!(count(&explicit, LoadUint), 0);
+    assert_eq!(count(&explicit, FloatToHalf), 0);
+}

+/// Where a program's constant table starts in its code: where its
+/// instructions end, unless a load at a fixed offset reads below that (then
+/// at `lowest`, the lowest offset any load or descriptor names). The
+/// program's constants follow its instructions, and a load at a computed
+/// offset reads them, not instruction bytes.
+fn code_table_start(p: &crate::ir::Program, addresses: &[&crate::resource::Address], lowest: u32) -> u32 {
+    let code_end = p
+        .blocks
+        .iter()
+        .flat_map(|b| &b.insts)
+        .map(|i| i.machine.offset + 4 * i.machine.words.len())
+        .max()
+        .unwrap_or(0) as u32;
+    let fixed_below = addresses.iter().any(|a| a.dynamic.is_none() && a.offset < code_end);
+    if fixed_below { lowest } else { lowest.max(code_end & !3) }
+}

     const S_ENDPGM: u32 = 0xBF81_0000;

+    /// A program's constant table starts after its instructions, unless a
+    /// load at a fixed offset reads below that.
+    #[test]
+    fn the_code_table_starts_after_the_instructions() {
+        // s_nop 0, s_endpgm, then 1,000 dwords of constants.
+        let mut words = vec![0xBF80_0000, S_ENDPGM];
+        words.extend(0..1000u32);
+        let code: Vec<u8> = words.iter().flat_map(|w| w.to_le_bytes()).collect();
+        let p = ir::build(&code, false, 256).unwrap();
+        let at = |offset: u32, dynamic: bool| resource::Address {
+            param: resource::CODE,
+            offset,
+            dynamic: dynamic.then_some(ir::Value(0)),
+        };
+        let (computed, fixed_after, fixed_inside) = (at(0, true), at(12, false), at(4, false));
+        // A load at a computed offset from the code's start: the table is
+        // the constants only, not the whole program.
+        assert_eq!(code_table_start(&p, &[&computed], 0), 8);
+        assert_eq!(code_table_start(&p, &[&computed, &fixed_after], 0), 8);
+        // A load at a fixed offset inside the instructions keeps the start.
+        assert_eq!(code_table_start(&p, &[&computed, &fixed_inside], 0), 0);
+        // Loads past the instructions only: from the lowest of them.
+        assert_eq!(code_table_start(&p, &[&fixed_after], 12), 12);
+    }

+//! A wave64 compute wave in one host wave32: each thread holds lanes n and n+32.
+use super::*;
+
+thread_local! {
+    pub(super) static PAIRED: std::cell::Cell<bool> = const { std::cell::Cell::new(false) };
+}
+
+/// The caller must widen lane branches and require full console waves.
+pub(crate) fn with_paired_lanes<R>(f: impl FnOnce() -> R) -> R {
+    struct Restore(bool);
+    impl Drop for Restore {
+        fn drop(&mut self) {
+            PAIRED.set(self.0);
+        }
+    }
+    let _restore = Restore(PAIRED.replace(true));
+    f()
+}
+
+#[derive(Default)]
+pub(super) struct LaneState {
+    pub(super) read_phis: HashSet<Value>,
+    pub(super) vals: HashMap<Value, Val>,
+    pub(super) floats: HashMap<Value, V>,
+    pub(super) bools: HashMap<(usize, Bool), V>,
+    pub(super) lane_bits: HashMap<Value, V>,
+    pub(super) lane_fail: HashMap<Value, Why>,
+    pub(super) mask_phis: HashMap<Value, V>,
+    pub(super) pending_masks: Vec<(V, usize, usize)>,
+    pub(super) data_phis: Vec<(V, usize, usize)>,
+    pub(super) handles: HashMap<(usize, Site, u8, bool, (u32, u32)), V>,
+    pub(super) strides: HashMap<(usize, Site, u8), V>,
+    pub(super) clane: Option<V>,
+}
+
+pub(super) fn lane_scalars(p: &Program, res: &Resources) -> HashSet<Value> {
+    let mut own: HashSet<Value> = res.waterfall.reads.iter().copied().collect();
+    if own.is_empty() {
+        return own;
+    }
+    let order = crate::ir::reverse_postorder(&p.blocks);
+    loop {
+        let before = own.len();
+        for &b in &order {
+            let block = &p.blocks[b];
+            for phi in &block.phis {
+                if !matches!(phi.reg, Reg::V(_)) && phi.args.iter().any(|a| own.contains(a)) {
+                    own.insert(phi.value);
+                }
+            }
+            for inst in &block.insts {
+                if matches!(inst.fields, Fields::Sop { .. } | Fields::Smem { .. })
+                    && inst.reads.iter().any(|r| own.contains(&r.1))
+                {
+                    own.extend(inst.writes.iter().map(|w| w.1));
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        if own.len() == before {
+            return own;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+impl<'a> Lower<'a> {
+    pub(super) fn swap_lanes(&mut self) {
+        let Some(t) = self.twin.as_deref_mut() else {
+            return;
+        };
+        std::mem::swap(&mut self.vals, &mut t.vals);
+        std::mem::swap(&mut self.floats, &mut t.floats);
+        std::mem::swap(&mut self.bools, &mut t.bools);
+        std::mem::swap(&mut self.lane_bits, &mut t.lane_bits);
+        std::mem::swap(&mut self.lane_fail, &mut t.lane_fail);
+        std::mem::swap(&mut self.mask_phis, &mut t.mask_phis);
+        std::mem::swap(&mut self.pending_masks, &mut t.pending_masks);
+        std::mem::swap(&mut self.data_phis, &mut t.data_phis);
+        std::mem::swap(&mut self.handles, &mut t.handles);
+        std::mem::swap(&mut self.strides, &mut t.strides);
+        std::mem::swap(&mut self.clane, &mut t.clane);
+        std::mem::swap(&mut self.read_phis, &mut t.read_phis);
+        self.upper = !self.upper;
+    }
+
+    pub(super) fn both<T>(
+        &mut self,
+        mut f: impl FnMut(&mut Self) -> Result<T, Why>,
+    ) -> Result<[T; 2], Why> {
+        let was = self.upper;
+        if was {
+            self.swap_lanes();
+        }
+        let lo = f(self);
+        self.swap_lanes();
+        let hi = f(self);
+        if !was {
+            self.swap_lanes();
+        }
+        Ok([lo?, hi?])
+    }
+
+    pub(super) fn put_lanes(&mut self, v: Value, lo: Val, hi: Val) {
+        let (mine, other) = if self.upper { (hi, lo) } else { (lo, hi) };
+        self.vals.insert(v, mine);
+        if let Some(t) = self.twin.as_deref_mut() {
+            t.vals.insert(v, other);
+        }
+    }
+
+    pub(super) fn block_phis(&mut self, b: usize) {
+        let p = self.x.p;
+        for (i, phi) in p.blocks[b].phis.iter().enumerate() {
+            let val = match phi.reg {
+                Reg::S(c) if c == EXEC || c == EXEC + 1 => Val::Missing("a lane mask"),
+                _ if self.bad.contains_key(&phi.value) => Val::Missing(self.bad[&phi.value]),
+                Reg::Scc => {
+                    let v = self.s.m.phi(self.cur, self.s.t.i1);
+                    self.data_phis.push((v, b, i));
+                    Val::Bit(v)
+                }
+                _ => {
+                    let v = self.s.m.phi(self.cur, self.s.t.i32);
+                    self.data_phis.push((v, b, i));
+                    Val::I32(v)
+                }
+            };
+            self.vals.insert(phi.value, val);
+        }
+    }
+
+    pub(super) fn paired_section_inst(&mut self, b: usize, i: usize, start: usize) {
+        use super::super::wave::Shape;
+        const UNKNOWN: Why = "a whole-wave section step the simulation does not follow";
+        let inst = &self.x.p.blocks[b].insts[i];
+        for &(_, w) in &inst.writes {
+            let shape = match self.section_out.contains(&w) {
+                true => self.sections[&b][&start].shapes.get(&w).copied(),
+                false => None,
+            };
+            let made = match shape {
+                None => Err(UNKNOWN),
+                Some(
+                    sh @ (Shape::Active(..)
+                    | Shape::Prefix(..)
+                    | Shape::ActiveF64(..)
+                   ),
+                ) => self.paired_shape(sh),
+                Some(sh) => self.both(|l| l.shape(sh, false)),
+            };
+            match made {
+                Ok([lo, hi]) => self.put_lanes(w, Val::I32(lo), Val::I32(hi)),
+                Err(why) => self.put_lanes(w, Val::Missing(why), Val::Missing(why)),
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    pub(super) fn paired_shape(&mut self, shape: super::super::wave::Shape) -> Result<[V; 2], Why> {
+        use super::super::wave::{Op, Shape};
+        if let Shape::ActiveF64(lo, hi, mask, high) = shape {
+            return self.paired_sum64(high, lo, hi, mask);
+        }
+        let (op, src, mask, ident, prefix) = match shape {
+            Shape::Active(op, src, mask, ident) => (op, src, mask, ident, None),
+            Shape::Prefix(op, src, mask, ident, inclusive) => {
+                (op, src, mask, ident, Some(inclusive))
+            }
+            _ => return Err("a whole-wave section value of another shape"),
+        };
+        let &m = self
+            .x
+            .st
+            .masks
+            .get(&mask)
+            .ok_or("a wave reduction's untracked mask")?;
+        let c = self.cur;
+        let [lo, hi] = self.both(|l| {
+            let on = l.boolean(m, c)?;
+            let x = l.i32_of(src)?;
+            let id = l.s.m.i32(ident);
+            Ok(l.s.m.select(c, on, x, id))
+        })?;
+        self.s.flags |= flags::WAVE_OPS;
+        if op == Op::AddH {
+            if prefix.is_some() {
+                return Err("a wave prefix of an operation other than a sum");
+            }
+            let (lo, hi) = (self.half_tree(lo, 5), self.half_tree(hi, 5));
+            let r = self.join_half_sums(lo, hi);
+            return Ok([r, r]);
+        }
+        let float = matches!(op, Op::AddF | Op::MinF | Op::MaxF);
+        let (lo, hi) = if float {
+            (self.as_float(lo), self.as_float(hi))
+        } else {
+            (lo, hi)
+        };
+        let r = match prefix {
+            Some(inclusive) => {
+                let mine = self.host_wave_op(op, lo, float, Some(inclusive))?;
+                let theirs = self.host_wave_op(op, hi, float, Some(inclusive))?;
+                let below = self.host_wave_op(op, lo, float, None)?;
+                [mine, self.s.m.bin(c, Bin::Add, theirs, below)]
+            }
+            None => {
+                let lo = self.host_wave_op(op, lo, float, None)?;
+                let hi = self.host_wave_op(op, hi, float, None)?;
+                let r = self.join_halves(op, lo, hi, float);
+                [r, r]
+            }
+        };
+        Ok(if float { r.map(|x| self.as_int(x)) } else { r })
+    }
+
+    pub(super) fn host_wave_op(
+        &mut self,
+        op: super::super::wave::Op,
+        arg: V,
+        float: bool,
+        prefix: Option<bool>,
+    ) -> Result<V, Why> {
+        use super::super::wave::Op;
+        let c = self.cur;
+        let (i32t, f32t) = (self.s.t.i32, self.s.t.f32);
+        let ty = if float { f32t } else { i32t };
+        let sfx = if float { "f32" } else { "i32" };
+        Ok(match (op, prefix) {
+            (Op::AddU | Op::AddF, Some(inclusive)) => {
+                let (k0, k1) = (self.s.m.i8(0), self.s.m.i8(1));
+                let r = self.s.op(
+                    c,
+                    &format!("wavePrefixOp.{sfx}"),
+                    Attr::NoUnwind,
+                    ty,
+                    121,
+                    &[arg, k0, k1],
+                );
+                if inclusive {
+                    self.s.m.bin(c, Bin::Add, r, arg)
+                } else {
+                    r
+                }
+            }
+            (_, Some(_)) => return Err("a wave prefix of an operation other than a sum"),
+            (Op::Or | Op::And | Op::Xor, None) => {
+                let k = self.s.m.i8(match op {
+                    Op::And => 0,
+                    Op::Or => 1,
+                    _ => 2,
+                });
+                self.s
+                    .op(c, "waveActiveBit.i32", Attr::NoUnwind, i32t, 120, &[arg, k])
+            }
+            (_, None) => {
+                let (code, unsigned) = match op {
+                    Op::AddU => (0, 1),
+                    Op::AddF => (0, 0),
+                    Op::MinU => (2, 1),
+                    Op::MaxU => (3, 1),
+                    Op::MinI | Op::MinF => (2, 0),
+                    _ => (3, 0),
+                };
+                let (k, u) = (self.s.m.i8(code), self.s.m.i8(unsigned));
+                self.s.op(
+                    c,
+                    &format!("waveActiveOp.{sfx}"),
+                    Attr::NoUnwind,
+                    ty,
+                    119,
+                    &[arg, k, u],
+                )
+            }
+        })
+    }
+
+    pub(super) fn join_halves(
+        &mut self,
+        op: super::super::wave::Op,
+        lo: V,
+        hi: V,
+        float: bool,
+    ) -> V {
+        use super::super::wave::Op;
+        let c = self.cur;
+        let ty = if float { self.s.t.f32 } else { self.s.t.i32 };
+        let sfx = if float { "f32" } else { "i32" };
+        match op {
+            Op::AddU | Op::AddF => self.s.m.bin(c, Bin::Add, lo, hi),
+            Op::Or => self.s.m.bin(c, Bin::Or, lo, hi),
+            Op::And => self.s.m.bin(c, Bin::And, lo, hi),
+            Op::Xor => self.s.m.bin(c, Bin::Xor, lo, hi),
+            _ => {
+                // FMax 35, FMin 36, IMax 37, IMin 38, UMax 39, UMin 40
+                let code = match op {
+                    Op::MaxF => 35,
+                    Op::MinF => 36,
+                    Op::MinU => 40,
+                    Op::MaxU => 39,
+                    Op::MinI => 38,
+                    _ => 37,
+                };
+                self.s.op(
+                    c,
+                    &format!("binary.{sfx}"),
+                    Attr::ReadNone,
+                    ty,
+                    code,
+                    &[lo, hi],
+                )
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    pub(super) fn half_tree(&mut self, x: V, steps: u32) -> V {
+        let c = self.cur;
+        let i32t = self.s.t.i32;
+        let lane = self
+            .s
+            .op(c, "waveGetLaneIndex", Attr::ReadOnly, i32t, 111, &[]);
+        let mut sum = x;
+        for step in 0..steps {
+            let bit = self.s.m.i32(1 << step);
+            let partner = self.s.m.bin(c, Bin::Xor, lane, bit);
+            let theirs = self.read_lane(sum, partner);
+            sum = self.add_halves(sum, theirs);
+        }
+        let (high, low, zero) = (
+            self.s.m.i32(0xFFFF_0000),
+            self.s.m.i32(0xFFFF),
+            self.s.m.i32(0),
+        );
+        let first = self.read_lane(x, zero);
+        let first = self.s.m.bin(c, Bin::And, first, high);
+        let sum = self.s.m.bin(c, Bin::And, sum, low);
+        self.s.m.bin(c, Bin::Or, first, sum)
+    }
+
+    pub(super) fn join_half_sums(&mut self, lo: V, hi: V) -> V {
+        let c = self.cur;
+        let total = self.add_halves(lo, hi);
+        let (high, low) = (self.s.m.i32(0xFFFF_0000), self.s.m.i32(0xFFFF));
+        let total = self.s.m.bin(c, Bin::And, total, low);
+        let first = self.s.m.bin(c, Bin::And, lo, high);
+        self.s.m.bin(c, Bin::Or, first, total)
+    }
+
+    pub(super) fn paired_inst(&mut self, b: usize, i: usize) -> Result<(), Stop> {
+        let inst: &'a Inst = &self.x.p.blocks[b].insts[i];
+        let name = inst.name();
+        let once = matches!(inst.fields, Fields::Sopp { .. })
+            || matches!(inst.fields, Fields::Smem { .. })
+                && (name.contains("store") || name.contains("atomic"));
+        if once {
+            self.inst(b, i)?;
+            self.mirror(inst);
+            return Ok(());
+        }
+        let cross = match name {
+            "v_readlane_b32" | "ds_bpermute_b32" => true,
+            // (not a waterfall loop's read, the lane's own dword: `Self::valu`)
+            "v_readfirstlane_b32" => !inst
+                .writes
+                .first()
+                .is_some_and(|w| self.x.res.waterfall.reads.contains(&w.1)),
+            _ => false,
+        };
+        if cross {
+            if let Err(why) = self.paired_cross(inst, b, i) {
+                for &(_, w) in &inst.writes {
+                    self.put_lanes(w, Val::Missing(why), Val::Missing(why));
+                }
+            }
+            return Ok(());
+        }
+        if matches!(inst.fields, Fields::Ds { .. })
+            && !name.starts_with("ds_swizzle")
+            && !name.contains("permute")
+        {
+            let write = !name.starts_with("ds_read");
+            if write || self.lds_last != Some(false) {
+                let (mode, void) = (self.s.m.i32(8), self.s.t.void);
+                self.s
+                    .op(self.cur, "barrier", Attr::NoDuplicate, void, 80, &[mode]);
+            }
+            self.lds_last = Some(write);
+        }
+        self.inst(b, i)?;
+        self.swap_lanes();
+        let r = self.inst(b, i);
+        self.swap_lanes();
+        r
+    }
+
+    pub(super) fn mirror(&mut self, inst: &Inst) {
+        let Some(t) = self.twin.as_deref_mut() else {
+            return;
+        };
+        for &(_, w) in &inst.writes {
+            if let Some(&v) = self.vals.get(&w) {
+                t.vals.insert(w, v);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    pub(super) fn paired_cross(&mut self, inst: &Inst, b: usize, i: usize) -> Result<(), Why> {
+        self.cross_lane(false)?;
+        let c = self.cur;
+        let (k31, k32, z) = (self.s.m.i32(31), self.s.m.i32(32), self.s.m.i32(0));
+        // a lane's value from the lower or upper value of the host lane `at` (of 0..63)
+        let pick = |l: &mut Self, lo: V, hi: V, at: V| {
+            let host = l.s.m.bin(c, Bin::And, at, k31);
+            let upper = l.s.m.bin(c, Bin::And, at, k32);
+            let upper = l.s.m.cmp(c, pred::NE, upper, z);
+            let (lo, hi) = (l.read_lane(lo, host), l.read_lane(hi, host));
+            l.s.m.select(c, upper, hi, lo)
+        };
+        match inst.name() {
+            "v_readlane_b32" => {
+                // the lane: a scalar or a constant
+                let lane = self.src(inst, 1)?;
+                let [lo, hi] = self.both(|l| l.src(inst, 0))?;
+                let r = pick(self, lo, hi, lane);
+                self.write_i(inst, r, None)?;
+            }
+            "v_readfirstlane_b32" => {
+                let [(lo, in_lo), (hi, in_hi)] = self.both(|l| {
+                    let x = l.src(inst, 0)?;
+                    let guard = l.guard(b, i)?;
+                    Ok(l.first_lane(x, guard))
+                })?;
+                // the first lane in EXEC is a lower one if there is one; with none, lane 0
+                let hi = self.s.m.select(c, in_hi, hi, lo);
+                let r = self.s.m.select(c, in_lo, lo, hi);
+                self.write_i(inst, r, None)?;
+            }
+            _ => {
+                // `ds_bpermute_b32`: each lane reads the lane its address names, which gives
+                // its data, or 0 outside EXEC
+                let Fields::Ds {
+                    dst: Some(Operand::Vector { reg, .. }),
+                    addr: Some(addr),
+                    data,
+                    offset0,
+                    offset1,
+                    ..
+                } = &inst.fields
+                else {
+                    return Err("a permute's operands");
+                };
+                let data = data.first().ok_or("a permute source")?;
+                let offset = u32::from(*offset0) | u32::from(*offset1) << 8;
+                let [(from_lo, x_lo, g_lo), (from_hi, x_hi, g_hi)] = self.both(|l| {
+                    let at = l.vgprs(inst, addr, 1)?[0];
+                    let x = l.vgprs(inst, data, 1)?[0];
+                    let guard = l.guard(b, i)?;
+                    let (o, two) = (l.s.m.i32(offset), l.s.m.i32(2));
+                    let at = l.s.m.bin(c, Bin::Add, at, o);
+                    let from = l.s.m.bin(c, Bin::LShr, at, two);
+                    let x = match guard {
+                        Some(g) => l.s.m.select(c, g, x, z),
+                        None => x,
+                    };
+                    Ok((from, x, guard))
+                })?;
+                let read = [
+                    pick(self, x_lo, x_hi, from_lo),
+                    pick(self, x_lo, x_hi, from_hi),
+                ];
+                let guards = [g_lo, g_hi];
+                let reg = *reg;
+                self.both(|l| {
+                    let n = usize::from(l.upper);
+                    l.write(inst, Reg::V(reg), read[n], guards[n]);
+                    Ok(())
+                })?;
+                return Ok(());
+            }
+        }
+        self.mirror(inst);
+        Ok(())
+    }
+
+    pub(super) fn paired_sum64(
+        &mut self,
+        high: bool,
+        lo: Value,
+        hi: Value,
+        mask: Value,
+    ) -> Result<[V; 2], Why> {
+        let &m = self
+            .x
+            .st
+            .masks
+            .get(&mask)
+            .ok_or("a wave reduction's untracked mask")?;
+        let c = self.cur;
+        let [x0, x1] = self.both(|l| {
+            let on = l.boolean(m, c)?;
+            let (dl, dh) = (l.i32_of(lo)?, l.i32_of(hi)?);
+            Ok(l.masked_double(on, dl, dh))
+        })?;
+        let (s0, s1) = (self.wave_sum_f64(x0), self.wave_sum_f64(x1));
+        let total = self.s.m.bin(self.cur, Bin::Add, s0, s1);
+        let (dl, dh) = self.split_double(total);
+        let x = if high { dh } else { dl };
+        Ok([x, x])
+    }
+
+    fn masked_double(&mut self, on: V, lo: V, hi: V) -> V {
+        let x = self.make_double(lo, hi);
+        let z = self.s.m.i32(0);
+        let zero = self.make_double(z, z);
+        self.s.m.select(self.cur, on, x, zero)
+    }
+
+    fn wave_sum_f64(&mut self, x: V) -> V {
+        self.s.flags |= flags::WAVE_OPS;
+        let c = self.cur;
+        let f64t = self.s.m.double();
+        let (sum, signed) = (self.s.m.i8(0), self.s.m.i8(0));
+        self.s.op(
+            c,
+            "waveActiveOp.f64",
+            Attr::NoUnwind,
+            f64t,
+            119,
+            &[x, sum, signed],
+        )
+    }
+
+    fn make_double(&mut self, lo: V, hi: V) -> V {
+        let c = self.cur;
+        let f64t = self.s.m.double();
+        self.s.flags |= flags::DOUBLES;
+        self.s
+            .op(c, "makeDouble.f64", Attr::ReadNone, f64t, 101, &[lo, hi])
+    }
+
+    fn split_double(&mut self, d: V) -> (V, V) {
+        let c = self.cur;
+        let i32t = self.s.t.i32;
+        let pair = self
+            .s
+            .m
+            .named_struct("dx.types.splitdouble", vec![i32t, i32t]);
+        let r = self
+            .s
+            .op(c, "splitDouble.f64", Attr::ReadNone, pair, 102, &[d]);
+        (self.s.m.extract(c, r, 0), self.s.m.extract(c, r, 1))
+    }
+}

+/// The mesh translation for the host launch stage. The normal translation
+/// keeps its zero-byte payload so the original pipeline remains a fallback.
+pub fn translate_with_mesh_launch(container: &[u8]) -> Result<HostShader, ShaderError> {

+fn with_mesh_launch_payload<T>(f: impl FnOnce() -> T) -> T {
+    struct Restore(u32);
+    impl Drop for Restore {
+        fn drop(&mut self) {
+            MESH_LAUNCH_PAYLOAD.set(self.0);
+        }
+    }
+    let _restore = Restore(MESH_LAUNCH_PAYLOAD.replace(4));
+    f()
+}

     io: &io::Io,
     res: &resource::Resources,
 ) -> Result<HostShader, ShaderError> {
+    let r = emit_forms(c, native, p, st, io, res);
+    // A console wave64 compute program on a host of 32-lane waves that no form above gives
+    // the host (it still asks for 64-lane waves, or does not translate): once more in the
+    // paired form, each console wave run by one host wave (`paired`; `VICTORY_WAVE_PAIRS=0`
+    // turns it off).
+    let refused = match &r {
+        Ok((_, wave)) => *wave == Some(64),
+        Err(e) => matches!(e, ShaderError::Unsupported { .. }),
+    };
+    if refused
+        && native.stage == HwStage::Cs
+        && native.wave64
+        && lower::host_max_wave() == 32
+        && pairs_on()
+    {
+        let again = paired(c, native, p, io, res);
+        if std::env::var_os("VICTORY_WAVE_WHY").is_some() {
+            match &again {
+                Ok((_, wave)) => eprintln!("WAVE64-RETRY paired lanes: wave {wave:?}"),
+                Err(e) => eprintln!("WAVE64-RETRY paired lanes: {e}"),
+            }
+        }
+        if let Ok((shader, wave)) = again
+            && wave != Some(64)
+        {
+            return Ok(shader);
+        }
+    }
+    r.map(|(shader, _)| shader)
+}

+/// Whether console wave64 compute programs may take the paired form ([`paired`]):
+/// `VICTORY_WAVE_PAIRS=0` turns it off (a test can choose for its own thread).

+    #[cfg(test)]
+    if let Some(on) = TEST_PAIRS.get() {
+        return on;
+    }
+    std::env::var_os("VICTORY_WAVE_PAIRS").is_none_or(|v| v != "0")
+}

 /// The translated program, and the wave size it requires of the host.
+fn paired(
+    c: &Container,
+    native: &NativeProgram,
+    p: &ir::Program,
+    io: &io::Io,
+    res: &resource::Resources,
+) -> Result<(HostShader, Option<u32>), ShaderError> {
+    let crate::native::StageIo::Cs(cs) = native.io else {
+        return Err(ShaderError::Unsupported {
+            offset: 0,
+            what: "paired lanes outside compute",
+        });
+    };
+    if !cs.threads.iter().product::<u32>().is_multiple_of(64) {
+        return Err(ShaderError::Unsupported {
+            offset: 0,
+            what: "paired lanes in a thread group that does not fill its console waves",
+        });
+    }
+    // every per-lane branch the wave's; again while that leaves another branch per lane
+    let mut wide = std::collections::HashSet::new();
+    let st = loop {
+        let st = structure::structure_wide(p, &wide)?;
+        let lanes: Vec<usize> = (0..st.exits.len())
+            .filter(|b| !wide.contains(b))
+            .filter(|&b| {
+                matches!(st.exits[b], structure::LaneExit::Branch { when, .. }
+                    if !st.bools.support(when).iter().all(|a| matches!(a, structure::Atom::Uniform(_))))
+            })
+            .collect();
+        if lanes.is_empty() {
+            break st;
+        }
+        wide.extend(lanes);
+    };
+    lower::with_paired_lanes(|| emit_program(c, native, p, &st, io, res))
+}

+//! Synthetic PR854/993 paired-wave regressions, usable without a device or game input.
+//! Native checks exercise translation and binary PSV/host contracts; only the Windows
+//! readback test executes generated shaders. Optional private inputs stay outside the repo.
+#![cfg(test)]
+
+use super::{HostShader, lower};
+use crate::dxbc::{Container, FourCC, u32_at};
+use crate::native::{
+    ComputeIo, HwStage, NativeProgram, ResourceType, RootParam, RootType, StageIo, UsedResource,
+};
+use crate::{ShaderError, io, ir, resource, structure};
+
+struct Overrides {
+    wave: Option<u32>,
+    pairs: Option<bool>,
+}
+impl Overrides {
+    fn new(wave: u32, pairs: bool) -> Self {
+        Self {
+            wave: lower::TEST_HOST_WAVE.replace(Some(wave)),
+            pairs: super::TEST_PAIRS.replace(Some(pairs)),
+        }
+    }
+}
+impl Drop for Overrides {
+    fn drop(&mut self) {
+        lower::TEST_HOST_WAVE.set(self.wave);
+        super::TEST_PAIRS.set(self.pairs);
+    }
+}
+
+const VCC: u32 = 106;
+const EXEC: u32 = 126;
+const LIT: u32 = 255;
+
+const fn v(n: u32) -> u32 {
+    256 + n
+}
+
+const fn k(n: u32) -> u32 {
+    128 + n
+}
+
+const fn sop1(op: u32, sdst: u32, src0: u32) -> u32 {
+    0xBE80_0000 | sdst << 16 | op << 8 | src0
+}
+
+const fn sop2(op: u32, sdst: u32, src0: u32, src1: u32) -> u32 {
+    0x8000_0000 | op << 23 | sdst << 16 | src1 << 8 | src0
+}
+
+/// A branch by `imm` dwords from the next instruction (or `s_barrier`, `imm` 0).
+const fn sopp(op: u32, imm: i16) -> u32 {
+    0xBF80_0000 | op << 16 | imm as u16 as u32
+}
+
+const fn vop1(op: u32, vdst: u32, src0: u32) -> u32 {
+    0x7E00_0000 | vdst << 17 | op << 9 | src0
+}
+
+const fn vop2(op: u32, vdst: u32, src0: u32, vsrc1: u32) -> u32 {
+    op << 25 | vdst << 17 | vsrc1 << 9 | src0
+}
+
+const fn vopc(op: u32, src0: u32, vsrc1: u32) -> u32 {
+    0x7C00_0000 | op << 17 | vsrc1 << 9 | src0
+}
+
+const fn vop3(op: u32, dst: u32, src0: u32, src1: u32) -> [u32; 2] {
+    [0xD400_0000 | op << 16 | dst, src0 | src1 << 9]
+}
+
+const fn ds(op: u32, vdst: u32, addr: u32, data: u32, offset: u32) -> [u32; 2] {
+    [
+        0xD800_0000 | op << 18 | offset,
+        vdst << 24 | data << 8 | addr,
+    ]
+}
+
+/// `buffer_store_dword v<data>, v<addr>, s[8:11], 0 offen offset:<offset>`
+const fn store(data: u32, addr: u32, offset: u32) -> [u32; 2] {
+    [0xE070_1000 | offset, 0x8002_0000 | data << 8 | addr]
+}
+
+/// `v<regs>` stored as each thread's dwords of the UAV, the thread's index (v0) times 16 on.
+fn stores(regs: [u32; 4]) -> Vec<u32> {
+    let mut w = vec![vop2(0x1A, 15, k(4), 0)]; // v_lshlrev_b32 v15, 4, v0
+    for (n, r) in regs.into_iter().enumerate() {
+        w.extend(store(r, 15, 4 * n as u32));
+    }
+    w
+}
+
+fn native_with(body: &[u32], wave64: bool, threads: u32, lds: u32) -> NativeProgram {
+    let mut words = vec![
+        0xBE85_0380, // s_mov_b32 s5, 0
+        0x3D << 26 | 0x02 << 18 | 8 << 6 | 2,
+        125 << 25, // s_load_dwordx4 s[8:11], s[4:5], 0
+    ];
+    words.extend_from_slice(body);
+    words.push(0xBF81_0000);
+    NativeProgram {
+        stage: HwStage::Cs,
+        wave64,
+        vgprs: 16,
+        layout: vec![
+            RootParam {
+                root: RootType::Constants,
+                table_offset_dwords: 0,
+                sgprs: 4,
+                base_register: 0,
+                user_data: 0,
+                slot: 0,
+            },
+            RootParam {
+                root: RootType::Table,
+                table_offset_dwords: 0,
+                sgprs: 1,
+                base_register: 0,
+                user_data: 4,
+                slot: 1,
+            },
+        ],
+        used: vec![UsedResource {
+            kind: ResourceType::Uav,
+            root: RootType::Table,
+            table_offset_dwords: 0,
+            lower_bound: 0,
+            stride: 0,
+            dwords: 4,
+            user_data: 4,
+            slot: 1,
+        }],
+        io: StageIo::Cs(ComputeIo {
+            rsrc2: 5 << 1 | lds.div_ceil(512) << 15, // 5 user SGPRs: s0..s4
+            threads: [threads, 1, 1],
+        }),
+        code: words.iter().flat_map(|w| w.to_le_bytes()).collect(),
+    }
+}
+
+fn lds_fixture() -> NativeProgram {
+    let mut w = vec![vop2(0x1A, 1, k(2), 0)]; // v_lshlrev_b32 v1, 2, v0: this lane's dword
+    w.extend(vop3(0x169, 2, v(0), k(7))); // v_mul_lo_u32 v2, v0, 7
+    w.extend([
+        vop2(0x26, 5, k(63), 0), // v_sub_nc_u32 v5, 63, v0: the mirror lane
+        vop2(0x1A, 5, k(2), 5),  // v_lshlrev_b32 v5, 2, v5: its dword
+        vop2(0x1B, 3, k(1), 0),  // v_and_b32 v3, 1, v0
+        vopc(0xC2, k(0), 3),     // v_cmp_eq_u32 vcc, 0, v3: the even lanes
+        sop1(0x24, 12, VCC),     // s_and_saveexec_b64 s[12:13], vcc
+        sopp(0x08, 4),           // s_cbranch_execz past the even lanes' arm
+        vop2(0x25, 4, LIT, 2),   // v_add_nc_u32 v4, 100, v2
+        100,
+    ]);
+    w.extend(ds(0x0D, 0, 1, 4, 0)); // ds_write_b32 v1, v4
+    w.extend([
+        sop2(0x15, EXEC, 12, EXEC), // s_andn2_b64 exec, s[12:13], exec: the odd lanes
+        sopp(0x08, 6),              // s_cbranch_execz past their arm
+        vop2(0x25, 4, LIT, 2),      // v_add_nc_u32 v4, 200, v2
+        200,
+    ]);
+    w.extend(ds(0x0D, 0, 1, 4, 0)); // ds_write_b32 v1, v4
+    w.extend(ds(0x36, 6, 5, 0, 0)); // ds_read_b32 v6, v5: an even lane's, from the other arm
+    w.extend([
+        sop1(0x04, EXEC, 12), // s_mov_b64 exec, s[12:13]
+        sop1(0x24, 12, VCC),  // s_and_saveexec_b64 s[12:13], vcc: the even lanes again
+    ]);
+    w.extend(ds(0x36, 6, 5, 0, 0)); // ds_read_b32 v6, v5: an odd lane's
+    w.push(sop1(0x04, EXEC, 12)); // s_mov_b64 exec, s[12:13]
+    w.extend(vop3(0x360, 14, v(6), k(5))); // v_readlane_b32 s14, v6, 5
+    w.extend(vop3(0x360, 15, v(6), k(40))); // v_readlane_b32 s15, v6, 40
+    w.extend([
+        sop2(0x00, 14, 14, 15), // s_add_u32 s14, s14, s15
+        vopc(0xC1, LIT, 6),     // v_cmp_lt_u32 vcc, 250, v6
+        250,
+        sop1(0x10, 16, VCC), // s_bcnt1_i32_b64 s16, vcc
+    ]);
+    w.extend(vop3(0x365, 7, VCC, k(0))); // v_mbcnt_lo_u32_b32 v7, vcc_lo, 0
+    w.extend(vop3(0x366, 7, VCC + 1, v(7))); // v_mbcnt_hi_u32_b32 v7, vcc_hi, v7
+    w.extend([
+        vop1(0x01, 9, 14),  // v_mov_b32 v9, s14
+        vop1(0x01, 10, 16), // v_mov_b32 v10, s16
+    ]);
+    w.extend(stores([6, 9, 10, 7]));
+    native_with(&w, true, 64, 256)
+}
+
+fn loop_fixture() -> NativeProgram {
+    let mut w = vec![
+        vop2(0x16, 1, k(6), 0),  // v_lshrrev_b32 v1, 6, v0: the console wave
+        vop2(0x1B, 2, k(63), 0), // v_and_b32 v2, 63, v0: the lane in it
+        vop2(0x1A, 11, k(2), 1), // v_lshlrev_b32 v11, 2, v1: the wave's dword
+        vop1(0x01, 12, k(0)),    // v_mov_b32 v12, 0
+        sop1(0x04, 22, EXEC),    // s_mov_b64 s[22:23], exec
+        vopc(0xD2, k(0), 2),     // v_cmpx_eq_u32 exec, 0, v2: lane 0
+    ];
+    w.extend(ds(0x0D, 0, 11, 12, 0)); // ds_write_b32 v11, v12
+    w.extend([
+        sop1(0x04, EXEC, 22),   // s_mov_b64 exec, s[22:23]
+        vop2(0x1B, 3, k(3), 2), // v_and_b32 v3, 3, v2
+        vop2(0x25, 3, k(1), 3), // v_add_nc_u32 v3, 1, v3
+        vop2(0x25, 3, v(1), 3), // v_add_nc_u32 v3, v1, v3: the rounds
+        vop1(0x01, 4, k(0)),    // v_mov_b32 v4, 0
+        vop1(0x01, 13, k(1)),   // v_mov_b32 v13, 1
+        sop1(0x04, 20, EXEC),   // s_mov_b64 s[20:21], exec
+        vop2(0x25, 4, v(2), 4), // loop: v_add_nc_u32 v4, v2, v4
+    ]);
+    w.extend(ds(0x00, 0, 11, 13, 0)); // ds_add_u32 v11, v13
+    w.extend([
+        vop2(0x25, 3, 193, 3),       // v_add_nc_u32 v3, -1, v3
+        vopc(0xC5, k(0), 3),         // v_cmp_ne_u32 vcc, 0, v3
+        sop2(0x0F, EXEC, EXEC, VCC), // s_and_b64 exec, exec, vcc
+        sopp(0x09, -7),              // s_cbranch_execnz loop
+        sop1(0x04, EXEC, 20),        // s_mov_b64 exec, s[20:21]
+    ]);
+    w.extend(ds(0x36, 5, 11, 0, 0)); // ds_read_b32 v5, v11: the wave's total
+    w.extend([
+        sop1(0x04, 24, EXEC),          // s_mov_b64 s[24:25], exec
+        vopc(0xD1, k(39), 2),          // v_cmpx_lt_u32 exec, 39, v2: lanes 40-63
+        vop1(0x02, 6, v(4)),           // v_readfirstlane_b32 s6, v4
+        sop1(0x04, EXEC, 24),          // s_mov_b64 exec, s[24:25]
+        vopc(0xD1, k(19), 2),          // v_cmpx_lt_u32 exec, 19, v2: lanes 20-63
+        vop1(0x02, 7, v(4)),           // v_readfirstlane_b32 s7, v4
+        sop1(0x04, EXEC, 24),          // s_mov_b64 exec, s[24:25]
+        vop1(0x01, 6, 6),              // v_mov_b32 v6, s6
+        vop1(0x01, 9, 7),              // v_mov_b32 v9, s7
+        0xD400_0000 | 0x346 << 16 | 6, // v_lshl_add_u32 v6, v9, 16, v6
+        v(9) | k(16) << 9 | v(6) << 18,
+        sopp(0x0A, 0),            // s_barrier
+        vop2(0x1D, 14, k(4), 11), // v_xor_b32 v14, 4, v11: the other wave's dword
+    ]);
+    w.extend(ds(0x36, 7, 14, 0, 0)); // ds_read_b32 v7, v14
+    w.extend(stores([4, 5, 6, 7]));
+    native_with(&w, true, 128, 512)
+}
+
+fn permute_fixture() -> NativeProgram {
+    let mut w = vop3(0x169, 1, v(0), k(3)).to_vec(); // v_mul_lo_u32 v1, v0, 3
+    w.extend([
+        vop2(0x25, 1, k(1), 1),  // v_add_nc_u32 v1, 1, v1
+        vop2(0x26, 2, k(63), 0), // v_sub_nc_u32 v2, 63, v0
+        vop2(0x1A, 2, k(2), 2),  // v_lshlrev_b32 v2, 2, v2: the mirror lane's address
+    ]);
+    w.extend(ds(0xB3, 3, 2, 1, 0)); // ds_bpermute_b32 v3, v2, v1
+    w.extend([
+        vop1(0x01, 4, LIT), // v_mov_b32 v4, 1000
+        1000,
+        vop1(0x01, 4, 0xFA), // v_mov_b32 v4, v1 row_shr:1 row_mask:0x6 bank_mask:0xf
+        0x6 << 28 | 0xF << 24 | 0x111 << 8 | 1,
+    ]);
+    w.extend(ds(0x35, 5, 1, 0, 0x401F)); // ds_swizzle_b32 v5, v1 offset:swizzle(BITMASK_PERM, xor 16)
+    w.push(vop1(0x01, 6, v(0))); // v_mov_b32 v6, v0
+    w.extend(vop3(0x361, 6, 0, k(45))); // v_writelane_b32 v6, s0, 45
+    w.extend(stores([3, 4, 5, 6]));
+    native_with(&w, true, 64, 0)
+}
+
+fn branch_fixture() -> NativeProgram {
+    let mut w = vec![
+        sop1(0x03, 7, LIT), // s_mov_b32 s7, 0x77
+        0x77,
+        vop1(0x01, 5, k(1)),    // v_mov_b32 v5, 1
+        vop2(0x16, 6, k(1), 0), // v_lshrrev_b32 v6, 1, v0
+        vop2(0x1A, 7, v(6), 5), // v_lshlrev_b32 v7, v6, v5
+        vopc(0xC4, 0, 0),       // v_cmp_gt_u32 vcc, s0, v0
+        sop1(0x24, 12, VCC),    // s_and_saveexec_b64 s[12:13], vcc
+        sopp(0x08, 20),         // s_cbranch_execz past the OR
+    ];
+    w.extend_from_slice(&super::wave::WAVE_OR_V7_TO_S7);
+    w.extend([
+        sop1(0x04, EXEC, 12), // s_mov_b64 exec, s[12:13]
+        vop1(0x01, 8, 7),     // v_mov_b32 v8, s7
+    ]);
+    w.extend(stores([8, 7, 6, 0]));
+    native_with(&w, true, 64, 0)
+}
+
+#[derive(Clone, Copy)]
+enum Form {
+    Paired,
+    Original,
+    Selected,
+}
+
+fn emit(
+    native: &NativeProgram,
+    _marks: bool,
+    form: Form,
+) -> Result<(HostShader, Option<u32>), ShaderError> {
+    let p = ir::build(&native.code, native.wave64, native.vgprs)?;
+    let st = structure::structure(&p)?;
+    let res = resource::track(&p, &native.layout, native.stage, Some(&st))?;
+    let io = io::raise(&p, native)?;
+    let bytes = crate::dxbc::build(&[]);
+    let c = Container::parse(&bytes)?;
+    match form {
+        Form::Paired => super::paired(&c, native, &p, &io, &res),
+        Form::Original => super::emit_program(&c, native, &p, &st, &io, &res),
+        Form::Selected => super::emit(&c, native, &p, &st, &io, &res).map(|s| {
+            let (_, min, max) = runtime(&s);
+            let wave = (min == max).then_some(min);
+            (s, wave)
+        }),
+
+    }
+}
+
+// Decode the emitted binary contract, not strings in the bitcode payload.
+fn runtime(shader: &HostShader) -> ([u32; 3], u32, u32) {
+    let c = Container::parse(&shader.dxil).expect("emitted container");
+    assert!(c.part(FourCC::DXIL).is_some(), "missing executable part");
+    let psv = c.part(FourCC::PSV0).expect("PSV0").data;
+    assert!(u32_at(psv, 0).unwrap() >= 48);
+    assert_eq!(psv[4 + 24], 5, "compute stage");
+    let word = |at| u32_at(psv, at).unwrap();
+    ([word(40), word(44), word(48)], word(20), word(24))
+}
+
+#[test]
+fn paired_synthetic_bodies_require_wave32_and_halve_the_group() {
+    let _scope = Overrides::new(32, true);
+    for native in [
+        lds_fixture(),
+        loop_fixture(),
+        permute_fixture(),
+        branch_fixture(),
+    ] {
+        let StageIo::Cs(cs) = &native.io else {
+            unreachable!()
+        };
+        for marks in [false, true] {
+            let (shader, wave) = emit(&native, marks, Form::Paired).expect("paired synthetic body");
+            assert_eq!(wave, Some(32));
+            assert_eq!(
+                runtime(&shader),
+                ([cs.threads.iter().product::<u32>() / 2, 1, 1], 32, 32)
+            );
+
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+#[test]
+fn paired_group_guards_use_total_threads_including_2d_groups() {
+    let _scope = Overrides::new(32, true);
+    for threads in [[32, 1, 1], [64, 1, 1], [96, 1, 1], [128, 1, 1], [8, 8, 1]] {
+        let mut native = permute_fixture();
+        let StageIo::Cs(cs) = &mut native.io else {
+            unreachable!()
+        };
+        cs.threads = threads;
+        let count = threads.iter().product::<u32>();
+        let result = emit(&native, false, Form::Paired);
+        if count % 64 == 0 {
+            let (shader, wave) = result.expect("complete console waves");
+            assert_eq!(wave, Some(32));
+            assert_eq!(runtime(&shader), ([count / 2, 1, 1], 32, 32));
+        } else {
+            assert!(matches!(
+                result,
+                Err(ShaderError::Unsupported {
+                    offset: 0,
+                    what: "paired lanes in a thread group that does not fill its console waves"
+                })
+            ));
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+#[test]
+fn paired_refuses_noncompute_before_lowering() {
+    let _scope = Overrides::new(32, true);
+    let mut native = permute_fixture();
+    let p = ir::build(&native.code, true, native.vgprs).unwrap();
+    let st = structure::structure(&p).unwrap();
+    let res = resource::track(&p, &native.layout, native.stage, Some(&st)).unwrap();
+    let io = io::raise(&p, &native).unwrap();
+    native.stage = HwStage::Vs;
+    native.io = StageIo::None;
+    let bytes = crate::dxbc::build(&[]);
+    let c = Container::parse(&bytes).unwrap();
+    assert!(matches!(
+        super::paired(&c, &native, &p, &io, &res),
+        Err(ShaderError::Unsupported {
+            offset: 0,
+            what: "paired lanes outside compute"
+        })
+    ));
+}
+
+#[test]
+fn paired_retains_all_host_annotations() {
+    let native = permute_fixture();
+    for marks in [false, true] {
+        let (mut original, wave) = {
+            let _scope = Overrides::new(64, false);
+            emit(&native, marks, Form::Original).expect("original wave64")
+        };
+        assert_eq!(wave, Some(64));
+        assert_eq!(runtime(&original), ([64, 1, 1], 64, 64));
+        let (mut paired, _) = {
+            let _scope = Overrides::new(32, true);
+            emit(&native, marks, Form::Paired).expect("paired wave32")
+        };
+        // All HostShader fields besides the executable remain the host's same contract.
+        original.dxil.clear();
+        paired.dxil.clear();
+        assert_eq!(paired, original);
+    }
+}
+
+#[test]
+fn exact_originals_stay_byte_identical_with_pairs_enabled_or_disabled() {
+    for (wave64, host_wave) in [(false, 32), (true, 64)] {
+        let mut native = permute_fixture();
+        if !wave64 {
+            native = native_with(&stores([0, 0, 0, 0]), false, 32, 0);
+        }
+        for marks in [false, true] {
+            let (original, required) = {
+                let _scope = Overrides::new(host_wave, false);
+                emit(&native, marks, Form::Original).expect("exact original")
+            };
+            for pairs in [false, true] {
+                let _scope = Overrides::new(host_wave, pairs);
+                let (selected, selected_wave) =
+                    emit(&native, marks, Form::Selected).expect("exact selection");
+                assert_eq!(selected_wave, required);
+                assert_eq!(selected, original);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+#[test]
+fn paired_choices_and_host_wave_are_in_the_translation_fingerprint() {
+    let fingerprint = {
+        let _scope = Overrides::new(32, true);
+        super::translation_fingerprint()
+    };
+    assert!(fingerprint.starts_with(env!("VICTORY_SHADER_SOURCE_HASH").as_bytes()));
+    for name in ["VICTORY_WAVE_PAIRS"] {
+        assert!(
+            env!("VICTORY_SHADER_SWITCHES")
+                .split(',')
+                .any(|s| s == name)
+        );
+        let mut entry = format!("{name}=").into_bytes();
+        if let Some(value) = std::env::var_os(name) {
+            entry.extend_from_slice(value.as_encoded_bytes());
+        }
+        entry.push(0);
+        assert!(
+            fingerprint.windows(entry.len()).any(|w| w == entry),
+            "{name}"
+        );
+    }
+    let _scope = Overrides::new(64, true);
+    assert_ne!(super::translation_fingerprint(), fingerprint);
+}
+
+#[test]
+fn overrides_restore_nested_choices() {
+    let before = (lower::TEST_HOST_WAVE.get(), super::TEST_PAIRS.get());
+    {
+        let _outer = Overrides::new(32, true);
+        assert!(super::pairs_on());
+        {
+            let _inner = Overrides::new(64, false);
+            assert!(!super::pairs_on());
+            assert_eq!(lower::TEST_HOST_WAVE.get(), Some(64));
+        }
+        assert!(super::pairs_on());
+        assert_eq!(lower::TEST_HOST_WAVE.get(), Some(32));
+    }
+    assert_eq!(
+        (lower::TEST_HOST_WAVE.get(), super::TEST_PAIRS.get()),
+        before
+    );
+}

+/// Opt in with a private directory containing only the desired .cdxil inputs.
+/// Never writes files, embeds private paths, or requires game data in the repository.
+#[test]
+fn optional_external_wave_shader_directory_translates_on_wave32() {
+    let Some(dir) = std::env::var_os("VICTORY_TEST_WAVE_SHADER_DIR") else {
+        eprintln!("VICTORY_TEST_WAVE_SHADER_DIR unset; external fixture check skipped");
+        return;
+    };
+    let mut paths: Vec<_> = std::fs::read_dir(dir)
+        .expect("external fixture directory")
+        .map(|e| e.expect("directory entry").path())
+        .filter(|p| p.is_file() && p.extension().is_some_and(|e| e == "cdxil"))
+        .collect();
+    paths.sort();
+    assert!(
+        !paths.is_empty(),
+        "explicit fixture directory has no .cdxil files"
+    );
+    let _scope = Overrides::new(32, true);
+    let mut failed = Vec::new();
+    for path in paths {
+        let name = path.file_name().unwrap().to_string_lossy();
+        let input = std::fs::read(&path).expect("read external fixture");
+        match super::translate(&input) {
+            Ok(shader) => {
+                let (threads, min, max) = runtime(&shader);
+                eprintln!(
+                    "{name}: translated, group={threads:?}, wave={min}..{max}"
+                );
+                // Unconstrained output (0..MAX) is also legal on a wave32 host.
+                if max == 64 || min > 32 || max < 32 {
+                    failed.push(name.into_owned());
+                }
+            }
+            Err(error) => {
+                eprintln!("{name}: translation failed: {error}");
+                failed.push(name.into_owned());
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    assert!(failed.is_empty(), "wave32 translation failed: {failed:?}");
+}
